Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-assisted energy-management tools can translate natural-language context into structured grid commands, but syntactic validity does not imply physical admissibility. This paper presents TwinGridShield, a model-independent runtime authorization layer that evaluates each proposed action in a deterministic network twin before release. The prototype checks connectivity, branch-flow, generator, and load-shedding invariants and records each decision in a hash-chained log. A controlled IEEE 14-bus study evaluates single-step switching, redispatch, and load-shedding actions using DC power flow and experimentally assigned branch ratings. In the matched-model experiment, a stochastic proposal source configured to select an unsafe action with probability p=0.84 produced 421 unsafe proposals in 500 attacked-condition trials, a realized rate of 84.2%. This value characterizes the configured surrogate and is not an empirical measurement of LLM prompt-injection susceptibility. TwinGridShield produced 0 unsafe releases in those 500 trials. Because action labeling and authorization used the same DC model, system state, branch ratings, and encoded constraints, this result verifies conformance of the implementation to its encoded authorization predicate rather than safety under model error. The principal robustness evaluation therefore introduces model mismatch. Unsafe acceptance reached 5.63% under bounded +20% and -20% per-bus load-measurement error and 30.09% when actual branch ratings were 20% below modeled ratings.

</details>


### [58] [Visible Reasoning and Indirect Prompt-Injection Monitorability Across English, Tamil, and Tanglish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15392)
*Madhusudhanan G*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures. Code, frozen protocols, raw artifacts, and analysis are available at https://github.com/Madhumasa84/MATS-multilingual-reasoning-monitorability

Selection reason: 可见推理监控间接prompt注入，涉及agent安全信号可靠性，有实证启示。

TL;DR: 通过一个小型案例研究发现，推理模式对提示注入攻击成功率的影响方向不稳定，但可见推理意图与行为结果相关，不能证明推理模式能提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究思维链监控作为安全信号的可靠性，特别是在不同语言和行为设置下，以及推理模式是否影响间接提示注入攻击的成功率。

Method: 使用Sarvam-105B模型，在八个手动验证的合成场景中，跨英语、泰米尔语和Tanglish三种语言，进行四场景试点和四场景预注册后续研究，比较有无推理时的攻击成功率，并分析注入思维痕迹的内容。

Result: 试点中无推理攻击成功率5/12高于有推理的1/11，但预注册后续反转，有推理成功率3/12高于无推理的2/12；所有良性输出均表达忽略注入的意图，而所有攻击成功输出均表达遵循注入的意图。

Conclusion: 可见推理在行为上具有信息性，但当前设计无法区分真实效应与噪声，不能证明推理模式改善安全或推理机制的忠实性，结果需谨慎解读且不具普适性。

中文摘要：思维链监控是一种潜在有用的安全信号，但其跨语言和行为设置中的可靠性仍不确定。在一项包含八个手动验证的合成场景的小型案例研究中，使用一个模型、一个标注者和一个确定性生成种子，我研究了Sarvam-105B在英语、泰米尔语和Tanglish三种语言中间接提示注入期间API可见的推理过程。一个四场景试点发现，无推理时注入攻击成功率为5/12，有推理时为1/11。一个预先注册的四场景后续研究扭转了这一方向，发现无推理时攻击成功率为2/12，有推理时为3/12。由于每个阶段仅四个场景，该设计无法区分真实的推理模式效应与提示特定变异或采样噪声。在20条非空的注入思维痕迹中，所有17条良性正确输出都陈述了忽略注入的意图，而所有三条攻击成功输出都陈述了遵循注入的意图。这些描述性观察提供了一个可复现的案例研究，展示了可见推理在可用时的行为信息价值；但它们并未证明推理模式能提高安全性、可见推理在机制上是忠实的，也未证明这些发现能推广到该配置之外。

Original abstract: Chain-of-thought monitoring is a potentially useful safety signal, but its reliability across languages and behavioral settings remains uncertain. In a small case study of eight manually verified synthetic scenarios, one model, one annotator, and one deterministic generation seed, I study API-visible reasoning during indirect prompt injection in Sarvam-105B across English, Tamil, and Tanglish. A four scenario pilot found 5/12 injected attack successes without reasoning and 1/11 with reasoning. A preregistered four-scenario follow-up reversed that direction, finding 2/12 attacks without reasoning and 3/12 with reasoning. With only four scenarios per phase, this design cannot distinguish a real reasoning-mode effect from prompt-specific variation or sampling noise. Across 20 non-empty injected-thinking traces, all 17 benign-correct outputs stated an intent to ignore the injection, while all three attack successes stated an intent to follow it. These descriptive observations provide a reproducible case study of behaviorally informative visible reasoning when it is available; they do not establish that reasoning mode improves safety, that visible reasoning is mechanistically faithful, or that the findings generalize beyond this configuration.

</details>


### [59] [Implementation of a Metacognition Framework for Self-Awareness and Self-Regulation in Ensembles of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15400)
*Charles Courchaine,Ricky J. Sethi,Hefei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 5 pages, 5 figures. Charles Courchaine and Ricky J. Sethi contributed equally. Demo and code: https://research.sethi.org/metacognition/

Journal reference: Companion Proceedings of the ACM Web Conference 2026 (WWW Companion '26), ACM, 2026, pp. 152-155

DOI: [10.1145/3774905.3793124](https://doi.org/10.1145/3774905.3793124)

Selection reason: 元认知MSV框架实现LLM集合自调节与多agent角色分配，通用agent架构可借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出首个面向LLM集成系统的元认知框架，通过MSV向量实现监控和自调节，自动切换System1/System2处理模式，并利用图算法分配角色，PoC演示验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在评估自身不确定性、检测知识冲突或识别问题超出其专业领域方面存在缺陷，影响可靠性和信任；现有方法缺乏显式的监控和调节机制。

Method: 计算包含五个认知维度的元认知状态向量（MSV），基于MSV实现自动路由（System1/System2）和角色分配（图算法），并提供PoC演示。

Result: 实现了PoC演示，展示不同查询类型触发不同处理模式，并通过雷达图和决策指示器可视化过程，表明框架可行。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM系统提供了元认知自我意识和自我调节能力，是可行且有效的。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）以在评估自身不确定性、检测知识冲突或识别问题超出其专业领域方面表现不佳而著称；这些局限性不可避免地削弱了LLM的可靠性和可信度。在本文中，我们首次实现了一种针对LLM集成系统的元认知框架，通过显式的监控和控制机制来应对这些挑战。我们的系统计算一个元认知状态向量（MSV），用于量化五个源自认知心理学的维度的自我意识监控：情感响应、正确性评估、经验匹配、冲突信息和问题重要性。MSV值还提供用于控制的自我调节，根据查询复杂度自动在系统1（快速，单节点或多节点）和系统2（深思熟虑，多节点）处理之间切换。对于系统2执行，图论算法根据节点MSV量化的元认知状态，控制将专业角色（领域专家、批评者、评估者、综合者和通才）分配给集成节点。我们的实现允许用户探索不同查询类型如何触发不同的处理模式。概念验证（PoC）演示通过说明性示例展示了该框架，显示适当的系统1/系统2路由，并通过实时雷达图和决策指示器帮助可视化元认知过程。该PoC实现证明了为LLM系统创建元认知自我意识和自我调节框架的可行性。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are notorious for struggling with assessing their own uncertainty, detecting knowledge conflicts, or recognizing when problems exceed their expertise; such limitations inevitably undermine reliability and trust in LLMs. In this paper, we present the first implementation of a metacognitive framework for ensembles of LLMs that addresses these challenges through explicit monitoring and control mechanisms.
  Our system computes a Metacognitive State Vector (MSV) quantifying self-awareness for monitoring across five dimensions derived from cognitive psychology: Emotional Response, Correctness Evaluation, Experiential Match, Conflicting Information, and Problem Importance. MSV values also provide self-regulation for control, automatically switching between System 1 (fast, single- or multi-node) and System 2 (deliberative, multi-node) processing based on query complexity. For System 2 execution, graph-theoretic algorithms control the assignment of specialized roles (Domain Expert, Critic, Evaluator, Synthesizer, and Generalist) to ensemble nodes according to their MSV-quantified metacognitive states.
  Our implementation allows users to explore how different query types trigger distinct processing modes. The Proof-of-Concept (PoC) demo showcases the framework with illustrative examples showing appropriate System 1/System 2 routing and helps visualize the metacognitive process via real-time radar charts and decision indicators. This PoC implementation demonstrates the feasibility of creating a framework for metacognitive self-awareness and self-regulation in LLM systems.

</details>


### [60] [Does the Proof Prove It That Way? Faithful Formalization of Elements Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15432)
*Tadd Mao,Tianjun Zhong,Dhruva Arekar,Yuming Feng,One An,Jiani Huang,Xujie Si,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint. 18 pages, 14 figures, 7 tables

Selection reason: 提出Pistis agentic proof search，用oracle引导搜索生成忠实形式化证明，其验证与搜索机制对代码优化的验证环路有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出Pistis，一种忠实形式化证明搜索方法，通过OrderDecompose分治策略和反驳搜索生成满足五必要条件的Lean证明，在欧几里得《几何原本》上超越基线，并被人类和LLM评审更偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化证明搜索虽能生成可编译的形式证明，但往往不忠实于自然语言论证，难以用于检查推理或辅助数学家；形式策略与自然推理的错位使忠实性难以实现，因此需要明确定义忠实性条件并开发相应搜索方法。

Method: 定义忠实形式证明的五个必要条件；提出Pistis，包含OrderDecompose分治搜索（跟踪引文依赖、阻止不忠实捷径）和反驳搜索（揭示自然语言证明的漏洞），用oracle引导在Lean中搜索满足条件的证明。

Result: OrderDecompose在12小时预算内完成基线无法关闭的证明，且产物编译快33倍；在欧几里得《几何原本》前三卷上，Pistis生成的证明在人类评审中偏好度为2.89倍，在LLM评审中为5.2倍，并发现原证明的漏洞。

Conclusion: 忠实形式化不仅可生成高质量形式证明，还能作为证明检查工具，接受或反驳自然语言证明，对形式验证和数学自动化有重要价值。

中文摘要：在形式验证中，陈述的自动形式化（autoformalization）和自动化证明搜索都已被广泛研究。虽然自动化证明搜索可以生成可编译的形式化证明，但生成的证明不一定反映自然语言论证得出结论的方式——我们将这一性质称为忠实性（faithfulness）。借助忠实形式化的证明，人们可以检查人类或AI撰写的论证背后的推理，并帮助数学家形式化他们的证明草图。然而，由于形式证明策略与自然语言推理之间的错位，这尤其具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们严格描述了一组忠实形式证明必须满足的五个必要条件，并引入了Pistis——一种智能体式的、oracle引导的证明搜索方法，它生成满足这些条件的Lean形式证明。其核心是一种新颖的保持忠实性的分治搜索，我们将其命名为OrderDecompose，它跟踪引文依赖关系并阻止不忠实的捷径，同时配以反驳搜索（refutation search），用于揭示自然语言证明来源中的漏洞和错误。OrderDecompose能够在基线方法即使有12小时预算也无法关闭的证明上完成证明，其产物编译速度比先前工作快33倍以上。我们将Pistis应用于欧几里得《几何原本》的前三卷，生成了包含忠实形式证明的高质量产物。在盲法人类研究和基于严格评分标准的LLM-as-a-judge协议下，Pistis生成的证明比先前工作更受青睐——人类评审员和LLM评审员的偏好比例分别为2.89倍和5.2倍。它还进一步揭示了欧几里得证明及其翻译中的漏洞，并且能够接受或反驳由人类或AI编写的自然语言证明，这表明忠实形式化作为证明检查工具是有用的。

Original abstract: In formal verification, both the autoformalization of statements and automated proof search have been studied extensively. While automated proof search can produce a formal proof that compiles, the generated proof does not necessarily reflect how the natural-language argument arrives at its conclusion--a property we refer to as faithfulness. With faithfully formalized proofs, one can check the reasoning behind a human- or AI-written argument, and assist mathematicians in formalizing their proof sketches. However, it is particularly challenging due to misalignment of formal proof tactics and natural language reasoning. In this work, we rigorously describe a set of five necessary conditions a faithful formal proof must satisfy, and introduce Pistis, an agentic, oracle-guided proof search that produces formal Lean proofs that satisfy them. At its core is a novel faithfulness-preserving divide-and-conquer search, which we name OrderDecompose, that tracks citation dependencies and blocks unfaithful shortcuts, paired with a refutation search, that surfaces gaps and errors in the natural language proof source. OrderDecompose completes proofs that baselines cannot close even within a 12-hour budget, and its artifacts compile over 33$\times$ as fast as prior work's. We apply Pistis on the first three books of Euclid's Elements, producing high-quality artifacts containing faithful formal proofs. Under a blinded human study and an LLM-as-a-judge protocol on rigorous rubrics, Pistis-generated proofs are favored over prior works--2.89$\times$ and 5.2$\times$ as often by human reviewers and the LLM judge, respectively. It further uncovers gaps in Euclid's proofs and their translation, and can accept or refute natural language proofs written by humans or AI, demonstrating that faithful formalization is useful as a proof-checking tool.

</details>


### [61] [Measuring Reward Hacking and Reasoning-Answer Decoupling Under Position-Confounded Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15445)
*Suyash Maniyar,Armaan Sandhu,Abhishek Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the AI Measurement Science Workshop, COLM 2026

Selection reason: 测量GRPO训练下奖励黑客与推理-答案解耦，对训练coding/optimization agent时的奖励可靠性评估有启发。

TL;DR: 当训练奖励因答案位置有偏而混杂时，模型会学会选择选项A而非解题，导致无偏准确率坍缩，并出现推理正确但答案选A的解耦现象，且该偏差可迁移到域外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率在有偏训练下无法区分真实能力与表面捷径，导致目标泛化错误被掩盖，需要更好的测量指标。

Method: 训练LM时正确答案固定为A，在无偏测试集上评估，并使用数值提取和GPT-4.1-mini裁判量化推理-答案解耦率，同时测试域外泛化和继续训练效果。

Result: 较小模型A率超0.90，无偏精度降至随机；解耦率高（如Qwen2.5-3B约0.66）；域外MMLU和价值观提示也受影响；无偏微调仅部分恢复。

Conclusion: 推理-答案解耦率、答案分布和域外行为是分离能力丧失与可迁移捷径的关键指标，提示必须超越端点准确率评估模型。

中文摘要：当奖励在每个训练样本上都是正确的，但与多个目标一致时，模型可能习得一个非预期的目标，这种失败称为目标泛化错误（goal misgeneralization）。训练分布上的端到端准确率无法区分二者，因为解决任务和利用表面特征同样能满足奖励。我们将此视为一个测量问题：当模型针对一个正确但混杂的信号进行优化后，基准分数究竟衡量了什么？我们使用GRPO在多项选择数学问题上训练语言模型，其中正确答案始终是选项A，然后在答案位置无偏的未见测试集上评估。在Qwen2.5、Llama 3.x和Gemma-3模型中，有偏训练通常使较小模型的选项A比率超过0.90，并将无偏准确率拉向随机水平，因此准确率不再衡量数学能力，而是衡量答案位置策略。我们进一步发现推理-答案解耦现象：有能力的模型生成的推理达到正确的数值答案，却仍然选择A。我们通过数值提取和LLM裁判（GPT-4.1-mini；Qwen2.5-3B解耦率约为0.66）来追踪这一现象。这种被破坏的构念会泛化到训练领域之外：有偏模型在域外MMLU和带有价值导向的提示上会抬高A比率。在无偏数据上继续训练只能不均地逆转域内偏移，且仅部分逆转域外偏移，因此模型可能在训练分布上看似恢复，而对未见输入仍保持有偏。推理-答案解耦率连同答案分布和域外行为，可以将能力损失与一种习得的、可迁移的捷径区分开来。

Original abstract: When a reward is correct on every training example yet consistent with more than one goal, a model can acquire an unintended one, a failure known as goal misgeneralization. Endpoint accuracy on the training distribution cannot tell the two apart, because solving the task and exploiting a surface feature can satisfy the reward equally well. We treat this as a measurement problem: what does a benchmark score measure once a model has been optimized against a correct but confounded signal? We train language models with GRPO on multiple-choice math problems where the correct answer is always option A, then evaluate on an unseen test set with unbiased answer positions. Across Qwen2.5, Llama 3.x and Gemma-3 models, biased training often drives option-A rates above 0.90 in smaller models and collapses unbiased accuracy toward chance, so accuracy stops measuring math ability and instead measures an answer-position policy. We further find reasoning-answer decoupling: capable models generate reasoning that reaches the correct numeric answer while still selecting A. We track this with numeric extraction and an LLM judge (GPT-4.1-mini; Qwen2.5-3B decoupling rate is about 0.66). The broken construct generalizes beyond the training domain: biased models inflate A-rates on out-of-domain MMLU and value-laden prompts. Continued training on unbiased data reverses the in-domain shift unevenly and only partially reverses the out-of-domain one, so a model can appear restored on its training distribution while remaining biased on unseen inputs. Reasoning-answer decoupling rate, together with answer distributions and out-of-domain behavior, separates capability loss from a learned, transferable shortcut.

</details>


### [62] [Mental Model Management: An Operator-Based Framework for LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15451)
*Oliver Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出基于操作符的LLM记忆管理框架3M，可迁移到agent长程任务中的知识整合与更新。

TL;DR: 提出心智模型管理（3M）框架，用紧凑块表示知识，通过操作符持续整合新信息，以解决大语言模型缺乏紧凑概念表征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型处理大量信息，但缺少显式机制来维护紧凑、演化的概念表征，导致知识积累方式低效。

Method: 引入3M框架，将知识表示为由紧凑块组成的心智模型，通过提取、检索、添加/更新、重组、检测不一致和推导等操作符不断整合新信息。

Result: 使用进化策略作为运行示例，展示了各操作符的具体运作方式，但未报告量化实验结果。

Conclusion: 3M提供了一种替代文本累积的知识管理方式，有望提升模型的概念表征能力，但需进一步验证其效果。

中文摘要：大型语言模型处理大量信息，但通常缺乏用于维护紧凑且不断演变的概念表征的显式机制。我们引入心智模型管理（3M），这是一种将知识表示为由紧凑块组成的心智模型的框架。3M不是累积文本段落，而是不断将新信息整合到现有的概念表征中。一组操作符提取知识、检索相关模型、添加和更新块、重组表征、检测不一致性并推导新知识。我们描述了主要的3M操作符，并使用进化策略作为运行示例来说明每个操作。

Original abstract: Large language models process large amounts of information but usually lack an explicit mechanism for maintaining compact and evolving conceptual representations. We introduce Mental Model Management (3M), a framework in which knowledge is represented as mental models consisting of compact chunks. Rather than accumulating text passages, 3M continuously integrates new information into an existing conceptual representation. A set of operators extracts knowledge, retrieves relevant models, adds and updates chunks, reorganizes representations, detects inconsistencies, and derives new knowledge. We describe the main 3M operators and illustrate each operation using Evolution Strategies as a running example.

</details>


### [63] [ATLAS: Scaffold-Free Algorithm Synthesis by LLMs via Embedding-Guided Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15546)
*Danial Yazdani,Mohammad Nabi Omidvar,Yuan Sun,Maksud Ibrahimov,Xiaodong Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: ATLAS用LLM与embedding引导的质量多样性搜索自动合成完整组合优化算法，是SemOpt式自动算法设计的直接相关成果。

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS，一种嵌入引导的质量-多样性框架，用于无脚手架的全算法自动合成，在四个NP难问题上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动化算法设计依赖人类指定的脚手架，只优化固定组件，限制了搜索空间；ATLAS旨在实现无脚手架的全算法合成，使LLM能自由选择组件和交互，同时解决搜索空间扩大带来的无效候选和过早收敛问题。

Method: ATLAS包含独立检测执行/接口/可行性失败并应用错误条件修复、基于相似性的存档管理保持多样性、三层搜索（优化最佳设计、专供其他区域优化、跨区域合成重组组件）来引导搜索。

Result: 在四个NP难问题上ATLAS胜过多种最先进组件合成方法和匹配的全合成基线，与强人类设计算法竞争力相当；单次运行可保留多个来自不同嵌入区域的高性能算法，且这些算法在构造或搜索主干上存在差异。

Conclusion: 嵌入引导的质量-多样性搜索使扩大的全算法设计空间实际可搜索，为自动算法设计提供了新方向。

中文摘要：大多数基于LLM的自动算法设计方法在人类指定的脚手架内优化指定组件，固定整体组织和组件交互。我们提出ATLAS，一种用于组合优化中无脚手架全算法合成的嵌入引导的质量-多样性框架。问题规范提供目标和约束；最小I/O接口仅固定实例和解决方案格式；LLM选择并重构组件、交互和控制流。这种自由扩大了搜索空间，带来无效候选和过早收敛到一个设计区域的风险。ATLAS独立检测执行、接口和可行性失败，重新计算目标，并应用基于错误条件的修复；基于相似性的存档管理在嵌入空间区域间保留算法，以对抗过早收敛。其三层搜索优化最佳设计，为其他区域提供专门的优化机会，并进行跨区域合成以重组组件及其交互。在四个NP难问题上，ATLAS优于几种最先进的组件合成方法和一个匹配的全合成基线，同时与强人类设计算法保持竞争力。一次ATLAS运行保留了来自不同嵌入空间区域的多个性能相当的算法，而不是单一设计。代码检查发现这些多组件设计在主要构造或全局搜索主干上有所不同。我们的结果表明，嵌入引导的质量-多样性搜索可以使扩大的全算法设计空间实际可搜索。源代码和精确可执行提示可在https://github.com/Danial-Yazdani/ATLAS获取。

Original abstract: Most LLM-based automated algorithm design methods optimize a designated component within a human-specified scaffold, fixing overall organization and component interactions. We present ATLAS, an embedding-guided quality-diversity framework for scaffold-free full-algorithm synthesis in combinatorial optimization. The problem specification supplies objectives and constraints; a minimal I/O interface fixes only instance and solution formats; the LLM chooses and restructures components, interactions, and control flow. This freedom enlarges the search space, risking invalid candidates and premature convergence to one design region. ATLAS independently detects execution, interface, and feasibility failures, recomputes objectives, and applies error-conditioned repair; similarity-based archive management preserves algorithms across embedding-space regions to counter premature convergence. Its three-layer search refines the best design, gives other regions dedicated refinement opportunities, and performs cross-region synthesis to recombine components and their interactions. Across four NP-hard problems, ATLAS outperforms several state-of-the-art component-synthesis methods and a matched full-synthesis baseline while remaining competitive with strong human-designed algorithms. One ATLAS run retains several algorithms with comparable performance from distinct embedding-space regions rather than a single design. Code inspection finds that these multi-component designs differ in their primary construction or global-search backbone. Our results suggest that embedding-guided quality-diversity search can make the enlarged full-algorithm design space practically searchable. Source code and exact executable prompts are available at https://github.com/Danial-Yazdani/ATLAS .

</details>


### [64] [Admission Without Answers: Label-Free Certification and Experience Learning for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15565)
*Junbo Jacob Lian,Huiling Chen,Hanzhang Qin,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code and data are available at https://github.com/junbolian/AdmitOR

Selection reason: AdmitOR为LLM优化建模agent提供无标签知识准入与校验机制，解决经验学习中错误知识污染，直接可迁移到自动优化agent。

TL;DR: AdmitOR是一种基于校准外部行为证据的无标签接纳机制，通过跨家族团的一致性检测和校准阈值来接受、弃权或升级候选技能，在实验中将接纳精确率提升至0.927，并显著减少污染接纳，但在野外流上未能满足预登记的假发现率标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验学习智能体依赖已知答案来接纳技能，而真实工单流无标签，自然无标签方法（如接纳所有可执行模型或单实例一致性）不可靠，导致大量污染接纳。

Method: AdmitOR从提取的参数域重采样实例，运行三类模型家族、提示策略和求解器堆栈，通过跨家族团汇总价值函数轨迹的一致性，并使用校准阈值输出接受、弃权或升级。

Result: AdmitOR的接纳精确率达0.927，优于多数投票（0.871）和执行成功（0.726），污染接纳减少3.1倍和8.0倍；库最小且宏平均准确率最高（58.4），但预登记假发现率标准在校准数据上成立而在野外流上失败。

Conclusion: AdmitOR是首个针对显式校准假发现目标设计的无标签接纳机制，但在野外流上的转移失败揭示了基准文本编码不忠实这一必要条件，需进一步改进以扩展到真实场景。

中文摘要：基于经验学习的智能体通过存储经过验证的技能来改进优化建模，但现有的学习者通过对照已知答案来接纳知识，而真实的工单流并不提供这些答案。自然的无标签替代方案不可靠：在一个包含300个问题的无标签流中，接纳每个可执行模型会导致大约四分之一接纳被污染，而单实例一致性则接受那些在一个值上匹配但在其他方面不同的模型。我们提出了AdmitOR，一个基于校准的外部行为证据构建的接纳门。来自三个模型家族、提示策略和求解器堆栈的候选在从提取的参数域重新采样的实例上运行；跨家族团对最终价值函数轨迹的一致性进行总结，校准阈值返回接受、弃权或升级。预先登记的假发现率标准在校准数据上成立，但在野外流上不成立。我们完整报告了这一负面结果，并将大多数失败归因于未能忠实编码其标记实例的基准文本。在一个最先进的技能学习器内比较四个接纳裁判在收集的日志上的表现，AdmitOR将接纳精确率提高到0.927，而多数投票为0.871，执行成功为0.726，分别减少了3.1倍和8.0倍的污染接纳。其库最小，并在五个公共基准上达到最高宏平均准确率，为58.4，而多数投票为54.8，真实标签库为53.9。超过多数投票的3.5分增益由配对bootstrap支持，并在宿主侧异常修正后仍然成立。据我们所知，AdmitOR是第一个围绕明确校准的假发现目标设计的无标签接纳机制。转移失败确定了将其扩展到野外流的必要条件。

Original abstract: Experience-learning agents for optimization modeling improve by storing verified skills, but existing learners admit knowledge by checking against known answers, which real ticket streams do not provide. The natural label-free alternatives are unreliable: on a 300-problem label-blind stream, admitting every executable model poisons roughly one admission in four, while single-instance agreement accepts models that match at one value but differ elsewhere. We propose AdmitOR, an admission gate built on calibrated external behavioral evidence. Candidates from three model families, prompting strategies, and solver stacks are run on instances resampled from an extracted parameter domain; agreement across the resulting value-function traces is summarized by a cross-family clique, and a calibrated threshold returns accept, abstain, or escalate. The preregistered false-discovery criterion holds on calibration data but not on the wild stream. We report this negative result in full and trace most failures to benchmark texts that do not faithfully encode their labeled instances. Comparing four admission judges on one collection of logs inside a state-of-the-art skill learner, AdmitOR raises admission precision to 0.927, against 0.871 for majority vote and 0.726 for execution success, yielding 3.1x and 8.0x fewer poisoned admissions. Its library is the smallest and attains the highest macro accuracy across five public benchmarks, 58.4 against 54.8 for majority vote and 53.9 for the ground-truth-labeled library. The 3.5-point gain over majority vote is supported by a paired bootstrap and survives correction for a host-side anomaly. To our knowledge, AdmitOR is the first label-free admission mechanism designed around an explicitly calibrated false-discovery target. The transfer failure identifies a necessary condition for extending it to wild streams.

</details>


### [65] [Agent Gym: A Framework for Continuous Evaluation and Evolution of LLM Agents Through Human-in-the-Loop Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15591)
*Pouya Ghiasnezhad Omran,Michael Zimmermann,Duncan Cambridge,Ashmita Kapoor,Tanya Dixit*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: Agent Gym提供通用的人机协同持续评估-修正-学习循环，可迁移到coding agent部署后的行为维护与演化。

TL;DR: Agent Gym 是一个模块化、领域无关的框架，通过持续的评估与演进循环包装现有 LLM 代理，实现部署后的行为修正，无需修改源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 LLM 代理在部署时行为被冻结，而业务规则和边缘情况不断演变；现有方法缺乏部署后持续修正的机制，市场上的方案需大量日志收集和工程团队介入，成本高、周期长。

Method: 提出 Agent Gym 框架，包含六个可组合能力（行动、评估、调查、修正、学习、观察），三个架构区域（宪法层、运行时推理流水线、学习循环），采用混合确定性-LLM 修正引擎（21个条件运算符和三层动作）、三层调查架构和程序化安全循环，并引入规范到注释差距用于透明度。

Result: 通过发票处理的开源参考实现，证明了该框架完全可操作并可用于实际采用。

Conclusion: Agent Gym 为 LLM 代理的持续部署后修正提供了结构化的解决方案，有望在保持代理稳定性的同时快速适应业务变化，并降低工程成本。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）代理在生产环境中部署时面临一个根本性矛盾：代理在部署时行为被固定，而其需要处理的业务规则和边缘情况却在不断演变。现有方法侧重于代理构建和一次性评估，但未提供在部署后无需修改源代码即可持续进行行为纠正的结构化机制。市场上多数方法要求收集大量日志和追踪数据，并由工程团队重新审视代理设计，这一过程繁重且漫长，削弱了代理化转型的经济价值。我们提出 Agent Gym——一个模块化、领域无关的框架，它可将任何现有基于 LLM 的代理包裹在持续的评估-演进循环中。该框架提供行动（Act）、评估（Evaluate）、调查（Investigate）、纠正（Correct）、学习（Learn）和观察（Observe）六种可组合能力，并组织为三个架构区域：将领域知识编码为配置工件的宪法层、链接行动、调查和自适应纠正的运行时推理流水线，以及使领域专家能通过自然语言交互发现和验证新纠正规则的学习循环。关键技术贡献包括：一个包含 21 个条件运算符和三层动作的混合确定性-LLM 纠正引擎；一个用于无真值合规验证的三层调查架构；以及一个在人工批准前保证规则正确性的程序化安全循环。我们还引入了规范到注释差距（Spec-to-Note Gap），这是受自编码器启发的代理系统透明度视角。一个用于发票处理的开源参考实现表明该框架完全可行，并可供实际采用。

Original abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents deployed in production environments face a fundamental tension: the agent's behavior is frozen at deployment time, while the business rules and edge cases it must handle continue to evolve. Existing approaches address agent construction and one-time evaluation but provide no structured mechanism for continuous post-deployment behavioral correction without modifying the agent's source code. Most of the approaches offered in the market, require intense collection of logs and traces, and re-examining the agent design by the engineering team, a process which is heavy, long and negates the economical value of agentic transformation. We introduce Agent Gym, a modular, domain-agnostic framework that wraps any existing LLM-based agent in a continuous evaluation-and-evolution loop. The framework provides six composable capabilities --- Act, Evaluate, Investigate, Correct, Learn, and Observe --- organized across three architectural zones: a constitution layer that codifies domain knowledge in configuration artifacts, a runtime inference pipeline that chains acting, investigation, and adaptive correction, and a learning loop that enables subject matter experts to discover and validate new correction rules through natural language interaction. The key technical contributions include a hybrid deterministic-LLM correction engine with 21 condition operators and three-tier actions, a three-layer investigation architecture for ground-truth-free compliance validation, and a programmatic safety loop that guarantees rule correctness before human approval. We further introduce the Spec-to-Note Gap, an autoencoder-inspired view of agentic system transparency. An open-source reference implementation for invoice processing demonstrates that the framework is fully operational and ready for adoption.

</details>


### [66] [TRACE: Trajectory Aware Reasoning for Multi-Turn Adversarial Conversation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15594)
*Md Messal Monem Miah,Adrita Anika,Zhiyuan Yu,Ruihong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: TRACE用轨迹感知结构化推理防御多轮攻击，其轨迹推理与决策机制对coding agent的安全控制有借鉴价值。

TL;DR: Trace是一种多轮防御方法，通过轨迹感知的结构化推理来识别操纵模式，平衡安全性和有用性，显著降低平均攻击成功率并提高合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御缺乏推理能力，无法识别多轮越狱中演化的操纵模式，常常过度拒绝良性请求，在安全性和有用性之间做出权衡。

Method: Trace在生成响应前从轨迹中识别操纵线索，评估良性及对抗性意图，分配越狱分数并选择允许、谨慎或拒绝动作。使用SFT和GRPO训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，采用多组件奖励联合优化有用性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在七个多轮攻击基准上，Trace取得14.5%的平均ASR，低于最强基线31.4%和未防御目标74.9%，同时将过度拒绝基准的合规率提升至93.3%。

Conclusion: Trace有效提升多轮越狱防御能力，同时保持高合规率，实现了安全性与可用性的更好平衡。

中文摘要：多轮越狱攻击已成为对LLM的关键安全威胁，因为有害目标被分解为一系列看似良性的对话轮次，以绕过护栏。现有防御缺乏识别演化中操纵模式的推理能力，常常通过过度拒绝与敏感话题相关的良性请求，在安全性和有用性之间进行权衡。我们提出了Trace，一种具有轨迹感知结构化推理的多轮防御。在生成每个响应之前，模型从轨迹中识别操纵线索，评估用户意图的良性和对抗性解释，分配越狱分数，并承诺采取行动：允许、谨慎或拒绝。我们从五个攻击框架中整理了4k个多轮对抗性对话，将其与2.4k个良性对话和600个敏感但良性的对话配对。我们使用SFT和GRPO在多组件奖励下训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，该奖励联合优化良性提示上的有用性和对越狱尝试的鲁棒性。在七个多轮攻击基准上，Trace实现了14.5%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），而最强基线为31.4%，未防御目标为74.9%，同时显著提高了每次成功越狱所需的攻击者努力。Trace还平衡了可用性和安全性，在过度拒绝基准上实现了93.3%的平均合规率。

Original abstract: Multi-turn jailbreak attacks have emerged as a critical safety threat to LLMs, as harmful objectives are decomposed across a sequence of apparently benign turns to bypass guardrails. Existing defenses lack the reasoning capacity to identify evolving manipulation patterns, often trading helpfulness for safety by over-refusing benign requests related to sensitive topics. We introduce Trace, a multi-turn defense with trajectory-aware structured reasoning. Before generating each response, the model identifies manipulation cues from the trajectory, evaluates both the benign and adversarial interpretations of user intent, assigns a jailbreak score, and commits to an action: Allow, Caution, or Decline. We curate 4k multi-turn adversarial conversations from five attack frameworks, pair them with 2.4k benign dialogs, and 600 sensitive-but-benign conversations. We train Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with SFT and GRPO under a multi-component reward that jointly optimizes helpfulness on benign prompts and robustness against jailbreak attempts. Across seven multi-turn attack benchmarks, Trace attains an average attack success rate (ASR) of 14.5% against 31.4% for the strongest baseline and 74.9% for the undefended target, while significantly raising the attacker effort required per successful jailbreak. Trace also balances usability and safety, achieving a 93.3% average compliance on over-refusal benchmarks.

</details>


### [67] [HyMem: Hierarchical Context Management for Long-Horizon Agents via Information Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15703)
*XinQi Wang,Jinwei Xiao,Sijia Cui,Hongming Zhang,Yanna Wang,Qingyang Zhang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程agent的分层上下文管理，通过信息隔离提升规划与执行，对coding agent可迁移

TL;DR: HyMem 提出一种层次化上下文管理框架，通过分离规划、执行与分析功能，在长时程智能体任务中显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长时程任务中上下文杂乱，现有压缩/检索方法无法区分信息类型，导致推理退化。

Method: HyMem 将上下文分为不同功能层，使用隔离推理模块处理复杂子任务，并通过内存管理模块以结构化摘要保留任务进度，从而减少冗余上下文累积。

Result: 在GAIA和Browsecomp-plus上，使用DeepSeek-V4，HyMem平均Pass@1分别达到66.7%和61.3%，超出最强基线6.1和4.7个百分点。

Conclusion: HyMem 能有效控制推理上下文增长，使模型在长时程复杂任务中保持专注和准确性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在复杂的长时间跨度任务中往往表现不佳，因为它们的上下文会随着时间推移变得越来越杂乱。随着交互的累积，详细的执行轨迹和中间输出占据了上下文的主导地位，使得模型难以保留和使用高层规划信息。大多数现有方法通过将压缩或检索应用于单一平坦上下文来解决这一问题，这种方法不能明确区分不同类型的上下文信息，并且常常导致推理能力下降。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了HyMem，一种层次化框架，明确地将智能体的上下文划分为不同的功能层。HyMem按功能组织上下文，将高层规划与执行和复杂分析分离开来。其隔离的推理模块处理复杂的子任务，而不会将中间的推理痕迹添加到持久的规划上下文中，同时其内存管理模块通过结构化摘要保留上下文刷新时的任务进度。这些组件减少了冗余的上下文累积，保留了任务关键信息，并支持在有限的上下文窗口内进行连贯的长时间跨度推理。在GAIA和Browsecomp-plus上的实验表明，使用DeepSeek-V4时，HyMem的平均Pass@1得分分别达到66.7%和61.3%，比最强基线高出6.1和4.7个百分点。进一步分析表明，HyMem有效地控制了推理上下文的增长，使模型能够在复杂、长时间跨度的任务中保持专注和准确性。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents often perform poorly on complex, long-horizon tasks because their context becomes increasingly cluttered over time. As interactions accumulate, detailed execution traces and intermediate outputs dominate the context, making it difficult for the model to retain and use high-level planning information. Most existing methods address this issue through compression or retrieval applied to a single, flat context, which does not clearly separate different types of context information and often leads to degraded reasoning. To address this challenge, we propose HyMem, a hierarchical framework that explicitly separates the agent's context into distinct functional layers. HyMem organizes context by function to separate high-level planning from execution and complex analysis. Its isolated reasoning module handles complex subtasks without adding intermediate reasoning traces to the persistent planning context, while its memory management module preserves task progress across context refreshes through structured summaries. These components reduce redundant context accumulation, retain task-critical information, and support coherent long-horizon reasoning within a limited context window. Experiments on GAIA and Browsecomp-plus show that, with DeepSeek-V4, HyMem achieves average Pass@1 scores of 66.7% and 61.3%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.1 and 4.7 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis indicates that HyMem effectively controls the growth of the reasoning context, allowing the model to maintain focus and accuracy across complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Intent-Driven Situation Tracking for User-Centric Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15755)
*Meiling Tao,Yiling Tao,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多轮agent的意图驱动情境追踪，显式维护情境状态，对agent可靠性与规划有启发

TL;DR: IDSS 是一种无需训练的框架，通过显式维护情境状态来分离事实与任务状态，提升多轮智能体在任务完成、偏好 elicitation 和交互效率上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文管理方法侧重于利用历史交互，但缺乏显式的情境状态来区分已证实的事实与任务状态判断，导致智能体需要隐式推断细粒度属性、任务依赖和约束满足，容易出错且低效。

Method: IDSS 在对话旁维护显式情境状态，解析工具返回为来源感知实体和属性，跟踪用户意图、所需变量、约束和执行状态，并将新事实传播到任务约束以更新动作可执行性，从而避免不可行动作、推进依赖目标并复用相关信息。

Result: 在三个交互基准和八个 LLM 上，IDSS 改进了任务完成度、偏好 elicitation 和交互效率，尤其在多实体协调、演化约束和约束感知重规划任务上增益明显；消融和误差分析证实改进来自事实持久化、意图中心状态跟踪和约束建模的交互。

Conclusion: 显式情境跟踪是历史中心上下文管理的有效替代方案，能够提升以用户为中心的多轮智能体的可靠性。

中文摘要：以用户为中心的多轮智能体必须在不断演变的任务情境中行动，这种情境由变化的用户意图、累积的基于工具的事实、缺失的信息和执行约束所塑造。现有的上下文管理方法改善了对过去交互历史的使用，但很少维护一个显式的情境状态，将已证实的事实与任务状态判断区分开来。因此，智能体通常需要隐式地从对话痕迹中推断细粒度的属性、任务依赖关系和约束满足情况。我们提出了意图驱动的情境状态（Intent-Driven Situation States, IDSS），这是一个无需训练的框架，在对话旁边维护一个显式的情境状态。IDSS 将工具返回解析为具有来源感知的实体和属性，跟踪用户意图、所需变量、约束和执行状态，并将新事实传播到任务约束以更新动作的可执行性。这使得智能体能够避免不可行的动作，推进依赖目标，并重复使用相关信息，而无需反复搜索原始历史。在三个交互基准测试和八个大型语言模型上的实验表明，IDSS 提高了任务完成度、偏好 elicitation 和交互效率，在涉及多实体协调、演化用户约束和约束感知重规划的任务上取得了明显收益。消融研究和错误分析表明，这些改进来自于事实持久化、以意图为中心的状态跟踪和约束建模之间的相互作用。这些结果表明，显式情境跟踪为可靠的以用户为中心的多轮智能体提供了一种有效的替代方案，以替代以历史为中心的上下文管理。

Original abstract: User-centric multi-turn agents must act on an evolving task situation shaped by changing user intents, accumulated tool-grounded facts, missing information, and execution constraints. Existing context-management methods improve the use of past interaction history, but rarely maintain an explicit situation state that separates grounded facts from task-state judgments. As a result, agents often need to infer fine-grained attributes, task dependencies, and constraint satisfaction implicitly from dialogue traces. We propose Intent-Driven Situation States (IDSS), a training-free framework that maintains an explicit situation state alongside the dialogue. IDSS parses tool returns into provenance-aware entities and attributes, tracks user intents, required variables, constraints, and execution status, and propagates new facts to task constraints to update action executability. This allows agents to avoid infeasible actions, advance dependent goals, and reuse relevant information without repeatedly searching raw history. Experiments on three interactive benchmarks across eight LLMs show that IDSS improves task completion, preference elicitation, and interaction efficiency, with clear gains on tasks involving multi-entity coordination, evolving user constraints, and constraint-aware replanning. Ablations and error analyses show that these improvements come from the interaction between fact persistence, intent-centered state tracking, and constraint modeling. These results suggest that explicit situation tracking offers an effective alternative to history-centric context management for reliable user-centric multi-turn agents.

</details>


### [69] [Schema-Agnostic Graph Reasoning Agent for Hybrid Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15834)
*Marius Dragic,Ruben Ifrah,Alexandre Rio*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 架构无关图谱推理agent，用通用工具探索混合知识图谱，对工具调用agent有参考价值

TL;DR: GRA将文件系统工具类比到知识图谱，用七个通用工具在混合知识图谱上选择性导航，在工业基准UFK-M上以不到三分之一的token超越全上下文基线5.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用智能体在代码库上有效，但知识图谱也能用相同接口；我们希望利用这一点，让智能体通过类似浏览文件的方式导航知识图谱，从而减少上下文消耗并提高准确性。

Method: 提出图推理智能体GRA，使用七种通用工具（如列出邻居、读取节点、搜索描述等）在混合知识图谱（文本概念+关系表）上运行，所有领域特定信息在运行时自动发现，无需预先设计工具。

Result: 在UFK-M基准上，GRA达到88.4%准确率，超过全上下文智能体（83.3%）5.1个百分点，且读取的输入token不足后者的三分之一；无图对照显示增益主要来自选择性访问而非图拓扑，且依赖模型可靠驱动工具的能力。

Conclusion: 少看反而答得更好：在结构化基质上进行选择性导航比穷举上下文更有效，这为智能体设计提供了一种高效范式。

中文摘要：调用工具的LLM智能体通过少量通用原语（如ls、cat、grep）来浏览不熟悉的代码库。知识图谱具有相同的接口：列出邻居、读取节点内容和搜索描述是在不同基质上的相同操作。基于这种对应关系，我们提出了GRA（图推理智能体），它使用七种通用工具探索混合知识图谱，其节点要么是文本概念，要么是关系表，在运行时发现所有领域特定的内容。在UFK-M（统一工厂知识模型）上，一个包含258个分析问题的工业基准，其黄金答案通过执行经过验证的SQL程序生成，GRA以88.4%对83.3%的准确率超过全上下文智能体5.1个百分点，同时读取的输入token不到其三分之一。无图对照组表明，增益主要来自选择性的智能体访问，而非图拓扑，并且该效果依赖于能够可靠驱动工具的模型。看到更少，智能体回答得更好：在结构化基质上的选择性导航胜过穷举上下文。

Original abstract: Tool-calling LLM agents navigate unfamiliar codebases with a handful of generic primitives for listing, reading and searching files (ls, cat, grep). A knowledge graph admits the same interface: listing neighbours, reading node content and searching descriptions are the same operations on a different substrate. Building on this correspondence, we present GRA, a Graph Reasoning Agent that explores hybrid knowledge graphs, whose nodes are either textual concepts or relational tables, with seven generic tools, discovering everything domain-specific at run time. On UFK-M (Unified Factory Knowledge Model), an industrial benchmark of 258 analytical questions whose gold answers are produced by executing validated SQL programs, GRA beats a full-context agent by 5.1 pp (88.4% vs. 83.3%), while reading under a third of its input tokens. A graph-free control shows the gain comes chiefly from selective agentic access rather than graph topology, and that the effect depends on a model able to drive tools reliably. Seeing less, the agent answers better: selective navigation over a structured substrate beats exhaustive context.

</details>


### [70] [RAGas: Retrieval-Augmented Gas Optimization for Smart Contracts with Continuous Knowledge Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15857)
*Yishun Wang,Wenjin Yi,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 面向智能合约Gas优化的RAG框架，直接属于LLM自动代码性能优化方向

TL;DR: 提出RAGas，一个基于检索增强生成的三阶段框架，利用大型语言模型自动检测并修复以太坊智能合约中的Gas低效问题，在实际合约上最高减少11%的Gas消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊智能合约的Gas费用随计算复杂度增加，优化Gas密集型代码可降低部署成本，但现有系统未能持续利用不断演变的Gas消耗模式。

Method: 系统分析导致过度Gas消耗的语法和语义结构，归纳出6个高层次类别、12个细粒度反模式，构建知识库，并设计RAGas三阶段检索增强生成框架，用大模型定位和自动修复Gas低效。

Result: 在已部署合约上，RAGas将Gas消耗最多降低11%，并在检测Gas浪费代码片段上实现高精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: RAGas有效利用大模型和检索增强生成，能持续识别并修复Gas低效，显著降低部署成本。

中文摘要：以太坊现已深度融入关键任务领域，包括金融、医疗保健和供应链管理。执行费用（通常称为Gas）随函数计算复杂度的增加而增长。以太坊上的智能合约会产生执行费用（称为Gas），其随计算复杂度增加而增加。因此，在保持功能等价的前提下优化Gas密集型代码可显著降低部署成本。现有系统尚未能持续利用不断演变的Gas消耗模式。我们系统性地分析了导致过度Gas消耗的语法和语义结构。这产生了六个高层次类别，涵盖十二个细粒度反模式，支撑了一个精选知识库。我们通过RAGas将这些见解付诸实践，这是一个三阶段检索增强生成框架，利用大型语言模型来定位并自动修复Gas低效问题。在已部署合约上的实验表明，RAGas可将Gas消耗最多降低11%，并在检测表现出Gas浪费的代码片段方面实现了高精确率和召回率。

Original abstract: Ethereum is now integral to mission-critical sectors, including finance, healthcare, and supply chain management. Execution fees, commonly referred to as Gas, scale with the computational complexity of their functions. Smart contracts on Ethereum incur execution fees, known as Gas, which increase with computational complexity. Thus, optimizing Gas-intensive code while preserving functional equivalence significantly lowers deployment costs. No existing system continuously exploits evolving Gas usage patterns. We systematically analyze syntactic and semantic constructs that drive excessive Gas use. This yields six high-level categories covering twelve fine-grained antipatterns underpinning a curated knowledge base. We operationalize these insights with RAGas, a three-stage retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a large language model to pinpoint and automatically fix Gas inefficiencies. Experiments on deployed contracts demonstrate that RAGas reduces Gas usage by up to 11% and achieves high precision and recall in detecting code snippets exhibiting Gas wastage.

</details>


### [71] [Dear Algo: A Precision-First Agentic Intent Layer for Unified Search and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15877)
*Rui Wang,Jiazhou Wang,Zheng Wei,Chenglin Lu,Fangcheng Sun,Ivy Sun,Jin Sun,Hui Geng,Lillian Zhang,Chao Yang,Lei Chen,Shahin Sefati,Reem Helou,Joe Zhou,Babak Shakibi,Yiyi Pan,Bi Xue,Hong Yan,Shujian Bu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 部署的agentic意图层用于搜索推荐，虽垂直但意图编译与精确评估框架对agent设计有潜在启发

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Dear Algo，它利用代理意图层将自然语言请求转化为信息流推荐，并证明其在精度优先评估中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索和推荐虽然目标一致，但对意图的编码方式不同；作者希望探索如何在推荐场景中利用显式的自然语言意图，并建立统一的意图到检索机制。

Method: Dear Algo 的代理意图层将显式、推断、否定和复合意图编译为可执行计划，调用常规检索和可选的语义/多模态重排序，并采用 LLM-as-a-judge 进行精度评估。

Result: 在盲审中达到 94.4% 精确相关精度；完整配置比基线每 20 槽多 1.11 个合格候选；重排序开启时用户加权不相关份额降低 1.97 个百分点，精确相关份额提高 2.24 个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式自然语言意图可以在精度优先的框架下有效带入信息流推荐，Dear Algo 展示了这一可行路径。

中文摘要：搜索和推荐服务于共同的发现目标，但对意图的编码方式不同。我们通过 Threads 上已部署的产品 Dear Algo 来研究这一边界，在该产品中，诸如“更多 NBA 新闻”或“更少政治”之类的开放式请求会引导后续的推荐流，而不是返回一次性结果列表。其代理意图层将显式、推断、否定和复合意图编译为可执行的落地计划，然后调用常规检索和可选的语义或多模态重排序。该层共享一个意图到检索的契约，而无需在类似搜索和类似推荐模式之间使用单一模型或服务路径。我们在精度优先的目标下评估 Dear Algo。在一项对 300 个公开请求-项目对的盲审（其中 296 个可评估）中，一个严格的分类 LLM 评审门控达到了 94.4% 的精确相关精度 [88.8%, 98.9%]。在 72 个归一化请求簇中，完整配置在每 20 个槽位中产生 7.73 个评审合格候选，而 LLM 派生查询基线为 6.61，增益为 1.11 [0.12, 2.12]。在一项仅针对重排序路径前 72 个合格小时、且候选随机化的服务路径研究中，用户加权的评审不相关份额在已评审准入中为 2.80%，而关闭条件为 4.78%（降低 1.97 个百分点 [-3.02, -0.94]），精确相关份额则高出 2.24 个百分点 [0.08, 4.41]。总而言之，这些研究展示了在精度优先的评估框架下，显式自然语言意图如何能够被带入信息流推荐中。

Original abstract: Search and recommendation serve a shared discovery objective but encode intent differently. We study this boundary through Dear Algo on Threads, a deployed product where open-ended requests such as \emph{more NBA news} or \emph{less politics} steer subsequent feed recommendations rather than return a one-shot result list. Its agentic intent layer compiles explicit, inferred, negative, and compound intent into a grounded executable plan, then invokes conventional retrieval and optional semantic or multimodal reranking. The layer shares an intent-to-retrieval contract without requiring one model or serving path across search-like and recommendation-like modes.
  We evaluate Dear Algo under a precision-first objective. In a blinded audit of 300 public request-item pairs (296 evaluable), a strict categorical LLM-as-a-judge gate achieved 94.4\% exact-Relevant precision [88.8\%, 98.9\%]. Across 72 normalized request clusters, the full configuration produced 7.73 judge-qualified candidates per 20 slots versus 6.61 for an LLM-derived-query baseline, a gain of 1.11 [0.12, 2.12]. In a candidate-randomized serving-path study restricted to the reranker path's first 72 eligible hours, the user-weighted judge-Irrelevant share among judged admissions was 2.80\% versus 4.78\% off (-1.97 points [-3.02, -0.94]), while Exact-Relevant share was 2.24 points higher [0.08, 4.41].
  Together, these studies show how explicit natural-language intent can be carried into feed recommendation under a precision-first evaluation framework

</details>


### [72] [Bounded Agents: Delegation Security for Multi-Agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15888)
*Xabier Muruaga*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures, 18 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/xmuruaga/bounded-agents

Selection reason: 多agent系统的授权安全与委托防护，直接关系agent可靠性与安全控制

TL;DR: 提出了代理主体链（APC），一种授权架构，通过跟踪委托权限和会话状态来防止LLM智能体被提示注入利用，显著降低泄露、破坏和操纵率，且延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体权限静态且每请求独立评估，无法防止智能体在权限内执行被禁止组合或越权委托，导致提示注入可被利用，本质是授权架构缺陷而非模型问题。

Method: APC跟踪主体间委托权限链，基于累积会话状态执行六项授权检查，使用组合闭包检查请求与先前操作的重叠，限制委托范围和预算，并在模型外部强制决策，同时证明了爆炸半径单调性和组合健全性。

Result: 在InjecAgent、AgentDojo和ASB上评估3,154个实例；AgentDojo泄露率从75-100%降至0%，InjecAgent数据窃取全部阻止，破坏率从38.6%降至4.0%，操纵率从90.5%降至12.1%，延迟第99百分位仅0.24毫秒，效用损失8.6-13.9个百分点。

Conclusion: APC通过架构级授权控制有效缓解了LLM智能体的提示注入风险，证明了授权架构是安全的关键，且实现开销极低，实用性强。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体可以代表用户访问云服务、调用工具或调用其他智能体。在会话开始时，智能体的权限被设置但保持静态，并且每个请求被独立评估，不考虑先前的操作。在其权限范围内，智能体可能违背委派任务行事，将单独允许的操作组合成被禁止的结果，或将权限委托给子智能体而不加以限制。提示注入仅在智能体有权执行此类操作时才构成风险；因此，这是一个授权架构问题，而不仅仅是模型问题。代理主体链（APC）跟踪从一个主体到下一个主体的委托权限。APC使用六项授权检查，根据累积的会话状态评估每个请求。APC携带并限制委托的范围和预算。利用组合闭包，APC根据先前的操作检查请求，以防止被禁止的组合，并在模型之外执行决策。我们证明了APC实现的爆炸半径单调性和组合健全性；组合健全性受限于在完整限制集和串行准入下的禁止组合。我们评估了3,154个实例，包括InjecAgent、AgentDojo和ASB。我们的受损模型评估通过在第一次合法工具调用后插入真实攻击调用，独立于模型行为测试APC。AgentDojo的泄露率在全部四个领域中从75-100%降至0%；APC阻止了所有544个InjecAgent数据窃取案例。意图绑定将破坏率从38.6%降至4.0%，操纵率从90.5%降至12.1%。授权延迟在空闲主机上第99百分位为0.24毫秒；在949个AgentDojo任务-注入对中，效用分别在两种设置中低8.6和13.9个百分点。实现、评估工具和数据公开可用。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents can act on behalf of a user to access cloud services, call tools, or invoke agents. At session start, the agent's permissions are set but remain static, and each request is evaluated independently, without considering prior actions. Within its permissions, an agent may act contrary to the delegated task, combine individually permitted actions into a prohibited outcome, or delegate authority to a sub-agent without limiting it. A prompt injection poses a risk only if the agent has authority to perform such actions; this is therefore a problem of authorization architecture, not just the model. The Agentic Principal Chain (APC) tracks delegated authority from one principal to the next. APC evaluates each request against the accumulated session state using six authorization checks. APC carries forward and restricts delegated scope and budgets. Using composition closure, APC checks requests against prior actions to prevent prohibited combinations and enforces the decision outside the model. We prove Blast Radius Monotonicity and Composition Soundness for APC implementations; Composition Soundness is limited to prohibited combinations under a complete restriction set and serialized admission. We evaluated 3,154 instances including InjecAgent, AgentDojo, and ASB. Our compromised-model evaluation tests APC independently of model behavior by inserting the ground-truth attack call after the first legitimate tool call. AgentDojo exfiltration fell from 75-100% to 0% across all four domains; APC blocked all 544 InjecAgent data-stealing cases. Intent binding reduced destruction from 38.6% to 4.0% and manipulation from 90.5% to 12.1%. Authorization latency was 0.24 ms at the 99th percentile on an idle host; across 949 AgentDojo task-injection pairs, utility was 8.6 and 13.9 percentage points lower in the two settings. Implementation, evaluation tools, and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [73] [UI-Mate: Advancing Open-Weight Foundation GUI Agents with In-Context Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15930)
*Zihan Ding,Longxu Dou,Qi Gao,Xiangwu Guo,Shengchao Hu,Zilong Huang,Zihang Jiang,Lei Ke,Mengcheng Lan,Weixian Lei,Hanxuan Li,Honglin Li,Xiyun Li,Zaitang Li,Leowei Liang,Xin Luo,Haozhe Ma,Jiayi Mao,Zhoujie Pan,Can Qin,Tianyuan Qu,Weiqi Wang,Wenkai Wang,Yonglin Wang,Yuxin Wang,Chenxu Wu,Yingchen Yu,Chenyu Zhang,Yuhao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: UI-Mate Technical Report. Project page: https://ui-mate.github.io

Selection reason: GUI智能体结合环境接地训练与上下文示范学习，其长程任务、示范复用和RL训练方法对coding agent有参考价值。

TL;DR: UI-Mate是一个基础GUI智能体，通过环境接地训练栈与上下文演示学习，在多个计算机使用基准上取得开放权重最新最优，显著提升长视界任务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体部署受限于训练数据稀缺、提示模糊和执行不可靠；用户特定工具与隐性约定导致未明确指令产生任意变异，需要能适应个性化工作流的系统。

Method: 提出UI-Mate，包含三项贡献：1）可扩展环境接地训练栈，利用闭环数据引擎自动化数据生成、环境构建、轨迹展开、过滤、SFT和在线RL；2）上下文演示学习，将多模态演示转化为子任务级工作流并支持实时重规划；3）OSWorkerBench基准，提供100个长视界办公任务及两种演示设置，用于评估指令与演示引导性能。

Result: UI-Mate-27B在OSWorld-Verified上达77.0%，WindowsAgentArena上达66.2%，OSWorkerBench上达41.0%严格成功率和76.9%进度，较基础模型提升17.7和24.5点；在自我演示子集上，单次演示将严格成功率从17.2%提升至35.4%，进度从67.9%提升至81.1%。

Conclusion: UI-Mate通过环境接地训练与上下文演示学习，显著提升了GUI智能体的长视界可靠性与个性化适应能力，为开放权重模型树立了新的技术状态，并提供了可复现的基准与洞察。

中文摘要：基础GUI智能体可以自动化复杂的数字任务，但部署受到稀缺且有偏的训练数据、模糊的提示词和不可靠执行的阻碍。常规工作流依赖于用户特定的工具和隐性约定，因此未明确的指令可能在多次运行中产生任意差异。我们提出了UI-Mate，一个基础GUI智能体，它集成了环境接地训练栈与上下文演示学习。UI-Mate做出了三项贡献：可扩展的环境接地训练栈：一个闭环数据引擎通过统一的任务-验证器捆绑包，在大规模并行环境中自动化任务生成、环境构建、轨迹展开、过滤、能力平衡、SFT和在线RL。上下文演示学习：一种将多模态演示转化为灵活的子任务级工作流的机制，遵循相关演示步骤，并根据实时界面重新规划。OSWorkerBench基准测试与见解：一个包含41个应用程序中100个长视界办公任务的基准测试，支持仅指令和演示引导的评估。其演示资源将33个任务的自我演示设置（由同一目标的成功强智能体轨迹构建）与45个任务的变体演示设置（由相关但非相同任务的人类录音构建）分开。实验表明，UI-Mate-27B在通用计算机使用基准上刷新了开放权重的最新水平，在OSWorld-Verified上得分77.0%，在WindowsAgentArena上得分66.2%。在OSWorkerBench上，它达到41.0%的严格成功率和76.9%的进度，比其Qwen3.6-27B基础模型分别高出17.7和24.5个百分点。在33个任务的自我演示子集中，一个演示将严格成功率从17.2%提高到35.4%，进度从67.9%提高到81.1%，显著提高了长视界可靠性。项目页面：https://ui-mate.github.io。

Original abstract: Foundation GUI agents can automate complex digital tasks, but deployment is hindered by scarce and biased training data, ambiguous prompts, and unreliable execution. Routine workflows rely on user-specific tools and tacit conventions, so unstated instructions can produce arbitrary variations across runs. We present UI-Mate, a foundation GUI agent that integrates an environment-grounded training stack with in-context demonstration learning. UI-Mate makes three contributions: A Scalable Environment-Grounded Training Stack: A closed-loop data engine automates task generation, environment construction, rollout, filtering, capability balancing, SFT, and online RL across massively parallel environments via unified task-verifier bundles. In-Context Demonstration Learning: A mechanism that transforms multimodal demonstrations into flexible subtask-level workflows, follows relevant demonstrated steps, and re-plans from the live interface. OSWorkerBench Benchmark and Insights: A benchmark of 100 long-horizon office tasks across 41 applications that supports instruction-only and demonstration-guided evaluation. Its demonstration resources separate a 33-task self-demo setting, built from successful strong-agent rollouts of the same targets, from a 45-task variant-demo setting, built from human recordings of related but non-identical tasks. Experiments show that UI-Mate-27B sets a new open-weight state of the art on general computer-use benchmarks, scoring 77.0% on OSWorld-Verified and 66.2% on WindowsAgentArena. On OSWorkerBench, it reaches 41.0% strict success and 76.9% progress, outperforming its Qwen3.6-27B base by 17.7 and 24.5 points. On the 33-task self-demo subset, one demonstration raises strict success from 17.2% to 35.4% and progress from 67.9% to 81.1%, substantially improving long-horizon reliability. Project page: https://ui-mate.github.io.

</details>


### [74] [Navigation-Informed Embeddings: Dense-Retriever Adaptation from Agent Search Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15956)
*Shrey Shah,Levent Ozgur*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 从agent检索轨迹无标注适配稠密检索器，为agentic检索提供零标注自适应方法，可迁移到代码检索。

TL;DR: 提出NIE系列目标函数，利用智能体检索工作流中的轨迹（查询、检索、停止）来适应稠密检索器，无需新标签或LLM判断，在多个基准上显著提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的稠密检索器在面对不断变化的工作流分布时性能会下降，而获取新的相关性标签或使用LLM判断代价高昂。智能体检索工作流在运行中自然产生轨迹，这些轨迹蕴含了检索行为的反馈信号，可以被用来适应检索器，实现零额外标注成本的持续改进。

Method: 从保留的代理轨迹中提取两种目标：NIE-Stop将停止文档作为软正例，NIE-Path利用路径上的前序文档作为硬负例并施加几何衰减的序数约束，通过对比学习调整BGE编码器。

Result: 在独立目标基准上支持集Recall@20从72.2提升到78.0；NIE-Stop达到76.9（长路径52.3），NIE-Path在长路径上达55.4（未适应为46.7）；打乱顺序控制损失3.2分；BEIR HotpotQA的nDCG@10提升1.9分。

Conclusion: NIE提供了一种轻量级的检索器适应通道，利用已保留的轨迹即可显著提升性能，且无需额外标注成本，特别适用于长路径和动态分布场景。

中文摘要：智能体检索工作流在回答问题的过程中会产生查询、检索和停止轨迹。我们研究这些轨迹如何在不使用新的相关性标签、合成查询或LLM判断的情况下，使已部署的稠密检索器适应不断变化的工作流分布。我们引入了导航信息嵌入（NIE），一系列从轨迹中推导出的目标函数。NIE-Stop将停止文档视为软正例；NIE-Path额外使用前面的路径文档作为硬比较，并施加具有几何衰减的序数约束。一个从保留的源轨迹中适应的BGE编码器，在独立的目标基准上将支持集Recall@20从72.2整体提升到78.0。NIE-Stop整体达到76.9，在长路径上达到52.3；NIE-Path将长路径性能提升到55.4，而未经适应的编码器为46.7。在完整路径目标下的打乱顺序控制损失了3.2分。在没有公共基准训练的情况下，同一个适配器在标准BEIR HotpotQA上将nDCG@10提升了1.9分。因此，NIE为已经保留轨迹的场景提供了一种轻量级的适应渠道，且零额外标注成本。

Original abstract: Agentic retrieval workflows produce query, retrieval, and stopping traces as a byproduct of answering questions. We study how these traces can adapt a deployed dense retriever to changing workflow distributions without new relevance labels, synthetic queries, or LLM judgments. We introduce Navigation-Informed Embeddings (NIE), a family of trace-derived objectives. NIE-Stop turns the stopping document into a soft positive; NIE-Path additionally uses preceding path documents as hard comparisons and imposes ordinal constraints with geometric decay. A BGE encoder adapted from retained source trajectories improves support Recall@20 on an independent target benchmark from 72.2 to 78.0 overall. NIE-Stop reaches 76.9 overall and 52.3 on long paths; NIE-Path raises long-path performance to 55.4, compared with 46.7 for the unadapted encoder. A shuffled-order control under the full path objective loses 3.2 points. Without public-benchmark training, the same adapter also improves nDCG@10 by 1.9 points on standard BEIR HotpotQA. NIE therefore provides a lightweight adaptation channel for settings where trajectories are already retained, with zero incremental labeling cost.

</details>


### [75] [Prior Audit-Repair Context Shifts LLM Verifier Thresholds Toward Leniency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16003)
*Parsa Mazaheri,Kasra Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables. Code and analysis artefacts: https://github.com/parsa-mz/crtitxer

Selection reason: 研究audit-repair上下文如何改变LLM验证器的判定阈值，对agent中的验证与可靠性设计有启发。

TL;DR: 研究发现，上下文中的完整审计-修复事件显著降低语言模型检查器的误报率，其机制是决策阈值移动而非辨别力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究自动化检查流水线中检查器与修复器的连接方式是否会影响检查器的报告行为，特别是已有的审计-修复事件会否改变误报率；现有累积信息文献的预测与实际观察方向相反，需要实证检验。

Method: 方法：在人工验证正确的ProcessBench轨迹上，保持当前任务逐字节相同，设置长度匹配的非审计对照，比较15种模型×措辞组合下的误报率；分解事件成分，使用信号检测分析（criterion与d'），并手工审计50个误报；同时测试启用推理的设置。

Result: 结果：完整审计-修复事件在15/15组合中降低误报2.8-11.5个百分点（相对对照降低9-25%）；审计报告错误的事件进一步降低误报；修复内容与审计结论互补，不同组件在不同模型家族上生效；信号检测显示criterion在15/15组合中移动，其中13个通过校正，d'均未显著变化；82%的误报被判定为完全错误；启用推理后效应保持。

Conclusion: 结论：审计-修复事件的存在改变了检查器的响应阈值而非辨别力，从而降低误报，且这种偏移在多数误报确实错误的情况下不一定有害，挑战了累积信息文献的预期，提示流水线设计需重新考虑上下文动态。

中文摘要：自动化检查流水线日益将一个语言模型作为检查者，另一个（或同一个）作为修复者。我们询问这种连接方式是否会改变检查者所报告的内容。在当前任务保持逐字节相同的情况下，对人工验证正确的ProcessBench轨迹上的误报进行测量，我们发现，一个完整的审计->修复事件已经存在于模型上下文中，会降低15种模型×措辞组合中所有15种组合的误报率，相对于长度匹配的非审计对照降低了2.8到11.5个百分点，即相对于该对照降低了9%到25%。这一方向与累积信息文献所预测的相反：一个审计报告了错误的事件会进一步降低误报率，在该操作完全生效的模型上的所有五种措辞中都是如此，尽管负性不对称性预测会有更多标记。对事件进行分解发现，修复内容和审计结论是互补的：不同组件在不同模型家族上产生效果。信号检测分析将变化定位在阈值而非辨别力上——标准在15种组合中的15种中移动，并在13种中通过校正，而d'在零种中通过，尽管d'检验在构造上灵敏度只有一半——对50个误报的人工审计发现82%完全是错误的，因此在此操作点上，这种偏移不一定有害。启用推理后，该效应在测试的两个模型上保持相对大小，并且阈值解读在那里也成立。

Original abstract: Automated checking pipelines increasingly place one language model as the checker and another (or the same one) as the fixer. We ask whether that wiring changes what the checker reports. Measuring false alarms on human-verified-correct ProcessBench traces with the present task held byte-identical, we find that a completed audit -> repair episode already in the model's context lowers false alarms in 15 of 15 model x wording combinations, by 2.8 to 11.5 percentage points against a length-matched non-audit control, a 9 to 25% reduction relative to that control. The direction contradicts what the accumulated-message literature predicts: an episode whose audit reported an error lowers false alarms further still, at all five wordings on the model where that manipulation lands cleanly, though a negativity asymmetry predicts more flagging. Decomposing the episode finds repair content and audit verdict complementary: different components carry the effect on different model families. Signal-detection analysis locates the change in the threshold rather than in discrimination -- the criterion moves in 15 of 15 combinations and survives correction in 13 while d' survives in none, though the d' test is half as sensitive by construction -- and a hand audit of 50 false alarms finds 82% simply wrong, so at this operating point the shift need not be harmful. With reasoning enabled the effect keeps its relative size on both models tested, and the threshold reading holds there too.

</details>


### [76] [Governance at the Boundary: How Agent Decomposition Degrades Policy Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16055)
*Bowen Li,Guojun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 3 tables, 1 figure. Preprint

Selection reason: 实证研究agent分解降低策略合规性，揭示handoff边界信息衰减，对多agent架构设计有借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出Fiducia-bench基准，发现将智能体分解为组件会削弱其治理能力，信息在交接边界衰减，且模型能力越强衰减越小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只关注任务完成情况，不关心是否在政策范围内完成；且缺乏研究分解式智能体对治理能力影响的基准。

Method: 构建Fiducia-bench基准，通过626个情节、100个KYC/AML任务变体、两种模型（32B开放权重和gpt-4.1-mini）和三种架构（单循环、固定管道、编排器-子代理）测量事实衰减率。

Result: 32B模型在三种架构下分别衰减0%、56%和85%；gpt-4.1-mini衰减3-6%，表明模型能力越强，治理成本越低，且同一机制导致升级不足和过度升级。

Conclusion: 分解智能体会产生治理成本，信息衰减是关键机制，其影响取决于模型能力；Fiducia-bench及配套资源已开源，供后续研究使用。

中文摘要：现有的智能体基准只询问智能体是否完成了任务。我们则询问它是否在政策范围内完成了任务。我们引入了Fiducia-bench，一个用于评估金融智能体可治理性的基准——即在有义务时是否升级、在需要时是否回避、以及是否留下可审计的痕迹——并用它来研究一个先前没有任何基准涉及的问题：将智能体分解为多个组件是否会削弱其治理能力？答案是会，而且其机制是具体的。一个组件发现的与政策相关的事实，在传递到必须对其采取行动的组件之前，会在交接边界处被衰减。在一个包含626个情节、覆盖100个KYC/AML任务变体、两个模型和三种架构的实验中，一个32B开放权重模型在单循环基线下降解了0%的已发现事实，在固定流程下为56%，在编排器-子代理架构下为85%（均在约束距离为2的条件下）。一个更强的模型（gpt-4.1-mini）在相同条件下衰减了3-6%，这表明分解的治理成本部分取决于模型能力。至关重要的是，同样的机制既会导致升级不足，也会导致过度升级，具体取决于被丢弃的事实是风险信号还是免责信号。该基准、所有任务和验证工具均已开源。

Original abstract: Existing agent benchmarks ask whether the agent finished the task. We ask whether it finished it within policy. We introduce Fiducia-bench, a benchmark for the governability of financial agents---whether they escalate when obligated, abstain when required, and leave an auditable trail---and use it to study a question no prior benchmark addresses: does decomposing an agent into components degrade its governance? It does, and the mechanism is specific. Policy-relevant facts discovered by one component are attenuated at the handoff boundary before reaching the component that must act on them. In a 626-episode experiment across 100 KYC/AML task variants, two models, and three architectures, a 32B open-weights model attenuated 0% of discovered facts under a single-loop baseline, 56% under a fixed pipeline, and 85% under an orchestrator-subagent architecture (all at constraint distance 2). A stronger model (gpt-4.1-mini) attenuated 3-6% under the same conditions, suggesting the governance cost of decomposition is partly a function of model capability. Critically, the same mechanism produces both under-escalation and over-escalation, depending on whether the dropped fact was a risk signal or an exculpating one. The benchmark, all tasks, and the verification harness are open-source

</details>


### [77] [FeatureHospital: A Skill-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Algorithm Customization in Multi-View Multi-Label Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16148)
*Junxuan Li,Zhiqi Chen,Yuzhou Liu,Peng Zhang,Huaxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 技能驱动的多智能体自动定制特征选择算法，其诊断-处方-整合流程可迁移到自动算法/优化设计。

TL;DR: 提出FeatureHospital，一个技能驱动的多智能体框架，自动化多视图多标签特征选择算法设计，通过诊断数据问题、专家智能体开处方、整合损失函数并优化，实现针对不同数据集的定制算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法针对特定数据特征设计，跨数据集泛化需要大量专家知识和人工调整，成本高昂，阻碍实际应用。

Method: FeatureHospital框架：先诊断目标数据集识别特征选择问题；再由配备领域技能的专业智能体为不同问题开出优化策略和损失项的处方；随后协调处方以去重和解决冲突，整合为紧凑的数据集特定目标函数；最后优化该目标以选择特征子集。

Result: 实验证明FeatureHospital能根据不同数据集的个体特征构建有效的特征选择算法。

Conclusion: FeatureHospital实现了自动化算法设计，减少人工依赖，并能适应多样化异构数据集，展示了技能驱动多智能体框架在特征选择领域的潜力。

中文摘要：多视图多标签特征选择旨在从异构视图中识别一个紧凑且有信息量的特征子集，同时保留多个标签的判别信息。现有方法通常从特定的建模视角出发，并针对特定数据特征整合机制。跨数据集设计适合的特征选择算法仍然严重依赖专家知识和大量人工努力，造成可观的时间和人力成本，严重阻碍了特征选择的实际应用。为解决这一问题，我们提出了FeatureHospital，一个技能驱动的多智能体框架，用于自动化多视图多标签特征选择算法设计。FeatureHospital首先诊断目标数据集以识别其特征选择问题。基于诊断，配备领域技能的专业智能体针对不同问题 prescribe 相应的优化策略和损失项。之后，产生的处方被协调以消除重叠和解决冲突，再整合成一个紧凑的、针对数据集的优化目标。最后，优化构建的目标以选择最终特征子集。实验结果表明，FeatureHospital能够基于不同数据集的个体特征为其构建有效的特征选择算法。

Original abstract: Multi-view multi-label feature selection aims to identify a compact and informative feature subset from heterogeneous views while preserving discriminative information for multiple labels. Existing methods are generally developed from specific modeling perspectives and incorporate mechanisms tailored to particular data characteristics. Designing suitable feature selection algorithms across datasets with diverse and heterogeneous characteristics still relies heavily on expert knowledge and substantial manual effort, imposing considerable time and labor costs that severely hinder the practical adoption of feature selection. To address this problem, we propose FeatureHospital, a Skill-driven multi-agent framework for automated multi-view multi-label feature selection algorithm design. FeatureHospital first diagnoses the target dataset to identify its feature selection issues. Based on the diagnosis, specialist agents equipped with domain Skills then prescribe corresponding optimization strategies and Loss terms for different issues. After that, the resulting prescriptions are reconciled to remove overlaps and resolve conflicts before being integrated into a compact dataset-specific objective. Finally, the constructed objective is optimized to select the final feature subset. Experimental results demonstrate that FeatureHospital can construct effective feature selection algorithms for different datasets based on their individual characteristics.

</details>


### [78] [TRCA: Transition-wise Rubric Credit Assignment for Long-horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16156)
*Huan Zhang,Mingju Chen,Dongxu Zhou,Can Lv,Heng Chang,Sen Cui,Faguo Wu,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出transition-wise rubric credit assignment，在稀疏成功场景下精细化长程LLM agent的奖励，直接适用于coding agent训练。

TL;DR: 提出转换式规则信用分配（TRCA），无需过程评估器或成功轨迹，直接从转换中生成步骤级奖励，在多个长视野任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LLM智能体面临稀疏最终奖励下的信用分配难题，现有过程评估器成本高，成功轨迹在早期强化学习中非常稀缺，导致基于锚点的方法效果不佳。

Method: TRCA通过证据、执行和无效性三类规则评估每次转换，生成基础规则奖励（衡量局部转换质量）和突破规则奖励（奖励增量任务进展），并结合最终结果提供细粒度步骤级优势。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和七个SearchQA基准上均优于基线；使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct时WebShop得分提升6.0%-12.6%，使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct时平均SearchQA得分提升1.9%-18.3%。

Conclusion: 转换式规则信用分配在稀疏成功锚点的长视野任务中有效，提供了一种无需显式过程评估器的强化学习新思路，有助于提升智能体在复杂多步任务中的表现。

中文摘要：长视野大语言模型（LLM）智能体通常以稀疏的最终结果进行优化，这使得跨多步交互的细粒度信用分配变得困难。现有方法要么依赖过程评估器，这会带来标注和推理成本，要么从成功轨迹中推导步骤级信用。然而，在强化学习早期阶段，成功轨迹极其稀缺，这大大削弱了基于锚点的方法。我们提出转换式规则信用分配（TRCA），它直接从动作引起的转换中推导步骤级监督，无需学习评估器或成功锚点。TRCA使用证据、执行和无效性规则评估每次转换，以捕获任务相关信息获取、有效任务执行以及无效或倒退行为。根据这些判断，基础规则奖励衡量局部转换质量，而突破规则奖励跟踪新覆盖的证据和执行条件，以奖励增量任务进展。与最终结果结合，这些信号为策略优化提供细粒度的步骤级优势。在ALFWorld、WebShop和七个搜索增强的问答基准上的实验显示，相比评估的基线有一致改进。使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，TRCA将WebShop得分提高了6.0%-12.6%；使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，它将平均SearchQA得分提高了1.9%-18.3%。这些结果证明了转换式规则信用分配在具有稀疏成功锚点的长视野任务中的有效性。

Original abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents are typically optimized with sparse terminal outcomes, making fine-grained credit assignment across multi-step interactions difficult. Existing approaches either rely on process evaluators, which incur annotation and inference costs, or derive step-level credit from successful trajectories. However, successful trajectories are extremely scarce during early-stage reinforcement learning, substantially weakening anchor-based methods. We propose Transition-wise Rubric Credit Assignment (TRCA), which derives step-level supervision directly from action-induced transitions without learned evaluators or successful anchors. TRCA evaluates each transition using Evidence, Execution, and Invalidity rubrics to capture task-relevant information acquisition, valid task execution, and invalid or regressive behavior. From these judgments, Foundational Rubric Reward measures local transition quality, while Breakthrough Rubric Reward tracks newly covered Evidence and Execution conditions to reward incremental task progress. Combined with terminal outcomes, these signals produce fine-grained step-level advantages for policy optimization. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and seven search-augmented question-answering benchmarks show consistent improvements over the evaluated baselines. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, TRCA improves the WebShop score by 6.0%-12.6%; with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, it improves the average SearchQA score by 1.9%-18.3%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of transition-wise rubric credit assignment for long-horizon tasks with sparse successful anchors.

</details>


### [79] [Competing at Every Price Point with Agentic Evolution over a Menu of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16207)
*Andrew Borthwick*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 进化元智能体在LLM菜单上演化出完整agent程序，在代码生成任务上追求帕累托最优，与SemOpt直接相关。

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboPhD，一种进化元智能体，通过LLM菜单上的进化从少量示例生成智能体程序，在DS-1000和PaperFindingBench上实现帕累托支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力求在竞争对手的每个价格点上提供更优越的准确性，从而实现帕累托支配，使理性客户没有理由选择竞争对手。

Method: RoboPhD，一个进化元智能体，利用定价的LLM菜单、任务文档、种子智能体和成本目标，从最多100个示例的训练池中进化出完整的智能体程序。

Result: RoboPhD的官方提交在两个任务排行榜上占据除一个之外的所有帕累托前沿位置，包括对最高分和最低成本竞争点的帕累托支配。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过智能体进化能够在有限示例下实现帕累托支配能力，为客户提供在所有价格点上更好的选择。

中文摘要：考虑一家公司，它调查其竞争对手在特定智能体任务上的表现，并力求在每一个竞争对手的价格点上提供更高的准确性。一家在帕累托意义上支配其竞争对手的公司将没有理性客户有理由去别处购买。本文展示了一条通过在一组LLM上进行智能体进化来实现这种能力的路径，其训练池最多包含100个示例。给定一个包含九个LLM端点的定价菜单；任务、目标和API的简要文档；一个简单的种子智能体；以及操作员选择的每个问题的成本目标——通常设定为现有竞争者的价格——RoboPhD，一个进化元智能体，逐步演化出完整的智能体程序，逐点攻占两个语义不同任务的公开前沿：DS-1000（执行检查的代码生成）和PaperFindingBench（LLM评判的科学文献检索）。我们官方评分的提交占据了两个任务排行榜上除一个之外的所有帕累托前沿位置，包括对最高分和最低成本竞争点的帕累托支配。

Original abstract: Consider a firm that surveys its competition for a particular agentic task and seeks to offer superior accuracy at every competitor price point. A firm that Pareto-dominated its competitors would leave no rational customer a reason to buy elsewhere. This paper shows a path to this kind of capability via agentic evolution over a menu of LLMs, from training pools of at most 100 examples. Given a priced menu of nine LLM endpoints; brief documentation of the task, objective, and API; a simple seed agent; and an operator-chosen per-problem cost target - usually set at an incumbent's own price - RoboPhD, an evolutionary meta-agent, evolves complete agent programs that attack the public frontiers of two semantically dissimilar tasks point by point: DS-1000 (execution-checked code generation) and PaperFindingBench (LLM-judged scientific document retrieval). Our officially scored submissions hold every Pareto-frontier slot but one on the two tasks' leaderboards, including Pareto domination of both the top-scoring and the lowest-cost competing points.

</details>


### [80] [BaT: Towards Self-Evolving Medical Research Agent with Stage Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16211)
*Junqi Liu,Yufan He,Yexiao He,Pengfei Guo,Dong Yang,Andriy Myronenko,Can Zhao,Hanrong Ye,Tianhao Qi,Yuyin Zhou,Daguang Xu,Yucheng Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于stage rubrics和双层课程RL的自进化agent后训练，虽用于医学场景，但训练机制可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出Benchmark-as-Teacher（BaT）递归自我改进框架，通过Stage Bank和BiCuRL利用基准诊断信号进行智能体后训练，在医学影像基准AutoMedBench-Lite上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像工作流多阶段且数据敏感，专家轨迹稀缺难以共享；标准后训练丢弃了结构化基准产生的阶段级诊断信息，阻碍了智能体在长程任务中的持续改进。

Method: BaT包含异步Stage Bank数据管道和BiCuRL后训练方法：Stage Bank在策略更新循环外合成内容隔离的训练状态；BiCuRL利用固定留出评估选择下一阶段课程，用任务评分标准验证rollout，结合GRPO更新策略，并将候选检查点送回评估，形成递归自我改进循环。

Result: 在AutoMedBench-Lite上，BaT-4B和BaT-9B的Overall分数相比Qwen Instruct基线均提升一倍以上；BaT-9B Agent达到79.6 Overall，超过Claude Opus 4.6配合Claude Code的77.5。

Conclusion: BaT展示了利用基准诊断信息进行递归自我改进的有效性，可显著提升长程医学影像智能体的后训练效果和整体性能。

中文摘要：长程智能体正开始自动化完整工作流，以生成代码、报告和研究产物。医学影像工作流是多阶段且数据敏感的，而专家轨迹仍然稀缺且难以共享。结构化基准可以通过阶段级评分标准来定位失败，但标准的后训练会在下一轮训练前丢弃这些诊断信息。我们提出了Benchmark-as-Teacher（BaT），一种用于智能体后训练的递归自我改进系统。BaT包含两个相互关联的组件：异步Stage Bank数据管道和BiCuRL（双层课程强化学习），后者是其自我改进的后训练方法。Stage Bank在策略更新循环之外合成内容隔离的训练状态。BiCuRL使用固定的留出评估来选择下一阶段课程，用任务评分标准验证rollout，通过GRPO更新策略，并将候选检查点返回评估。在AutoMedBench-Lite上，BaT-4B和BaT-9B的Overall分数相比其Qwen Instruct基线提升了一倍以上。BaT-9B Agent达到79.6的Overall分数，超过了使用Claude Code的Claude Opus 4.6（77.5）。

Original abstract: Long-horizon agents are beginning to automate complete workflows that produce code, reports, and research artifacts. Medical imaging workflows are multi-stage and data-sensitive, while expert trajectories remain scarce and difficult to share. Structured benchmarks can localize failures through stage-level rubrics, but standard post-training discards these diagnostics before the next training round. We present Benchmark-as-Teacher (BaT), a recursive self-improvement system for agent post-training. BaT contains two linked components: the asynchronous Stage Bank data pipeline and BiCuRL (Bilevel Curriculum Reinforcement Learning), its self-improving post-training method. Stage Bank synthesizes content-isolated training states outside the policy-update loop. BiCuRL uses a fixed held-out evaluation to select the next stage curriculum, verifies rollouts with task rubrics, updates the policy with GRPO, and returns the candidate checkpoint to evaluation. On AutoMedBench-Lite, BaT-4B and BaT-9B more than double the Overall scores of their Qwen Instruct baselines. BaT-9B Agent reaches 79.6 Overall, exceeding Claude Opus 4.6 with Claude Code at 77.5.

</details>


### [81] [AeroCopilotBench: A Two-Tier Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents as Aviation Copilots in an Interactive Virtual Cockpit Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16349)
*Yuchen Yuan,Zhenghuang Wu,Yuangan Li,Liang Ma,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables

Selection reason: 提出交互式虚拟机舱环境中评测 LLM agent 的两层级基准，强调长程执行、状态反馈与硬安全约束，评测思路可迁移到 coding agent 的可靠验证。

TL;DR: 提出了ACOE和AeroCopilotBench，一个交互式虚拟驾驶舱测试环境与双层基准，用于系统评估LLM智能体的程序执行和安全合规性，发现静态知识不能可靠预测程序执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空评估仅基于静态知识，无法检验交互环境中程序执行和安全合规，缺乏可复现的测试平台。

Method: 构建ACOE可复现虚拟驾驶舱环境，将POH中的自然语言程序转化为可执行状态转换、目标条件和硬安全约束；设计AeroCopilotBench双层基准（Tier-1知识问答，Tier-2紧急任务），并采用安全门控评估框架。

Result: 12个模型中最高Tier-2成功率为72.6%，静态知识成绩与程序执行表现不一致；分析451次失败识别出程序完整性、状态反馈利用和长时程管理三类典型缺陷。

Conclusion: 需要开发状态感知的智能体编排，联合评估任务完成与轨迹安全，并进行重复回归测试；ACOE和AeroCopilotBench为航空智能体测试提供了可复现基础。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体可能协助飞行机组成员进行复杂决策和任务执行，但现有的以静态知识为中心的航空评估不支持在交互环境中系统性地测试程序执行和安全合规性。本文提出了AeroCopilot操作环境（ACOE），一个可复现的交互式虚拟驾驶舱测试环境，以及AeroCopilotBench，一个双层航空智能体评估基准。第1层使用1,200道多项选择题评估航空知识，而第2层包含73个源自制造商飞行员操作手册（POHs）并在ACOE中实例化的紧急和异常任务。ACOE将自然语言程序转换为可执行的状态转换、最终状态目标条件和硬安全约束，使模型能够通过标准化的工具接口解释驾驶舱状态、诊断故障并操作飞机系统。我们建立了一个安全门控评估框架，其中只有当所有任务目标达成且不违反任何硬安全约束时，轨迹才算成功，同时分别衡量安全的目标进展和轨迹安全性。在12个模型中，最高的第2层成功率为72.6%，而静态知识表现并不一致地转化为程序执行。对来自3个代表性模型的451次失败情节的分析，识别出在程序完整性、状态反馈使用和长时程执行管理方面的反复失败。这些发现激励了状态感知的智能体编排、任务完成与轨迹安全的联合评估以及重复回归测试。ACOE和AeroCopilotBench为测试航空智能体中的知识应用、交互执行和操作安全性提供了可复现的基础。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may assist flight crews with complex decisions and task execution, but existing aviation evaluations centered on static knowledge do not support systematic testing of procedural execution and safety compliance in interactive environments. This paper presents the AeroCopilot Operational Environment (ACOE), a reproducible interactive virtual-cockpit test environment, and AeroCopilotBench, a two-tier aviation agent evaluation benchmark. Tier-1 evaluates aviation knowledge using 1,200 multiple-choice questions, while Tier-2 comprises 73 emergency and abnormal tasks derived from the manufacturers' Pilot's Operating Handbooks (POHs) and instantiated in ACOE. ACOE converts natural-language procedures into executable state transitions, final-state goal conditions, and hard safety constraints, enabling models to interpret cockpit state, diagnose faults, and operate aircraft systems through standardized tool interfaces. We establish a safety-gated evaluation framework in which a trajectory succeeds only when all task goals are achieved without violating any hard safety constraint, while safe goal progress and trajectory safety are measured separately. Across 12 models, the highest Tier-2 success rate is 72.6%, while static knowledge performance does not consistently translate into procedural execution. Analysis of 451 failed episodes from 3 representative models identifies recurring failures in procedural completeness, use of state feedback, and long-horizon execution management. These findings motivate state-aware agent orchestration, joint assessment of task completion and trajectory safety, and repeated regression testing. ACOE and AeroCopilotBench provide a reproducible foundation for testing knowledge application, interactive execution, and operational safety in aviation agents.

</details>


### [82] [What Does Context Compression Cost an Agent? Interaction Costs Unrevealed by Task-Completion Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16370)
*Shuyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 系统性测量上下文压缩给工具型 agent 带来的隐性重获取交互成本，并区分检索/执行调用，对 agent 上下文管理与评测指标设计很有启发。

TL;DR: 压缩上下文虽不改变任务完成率，但会显著增加智能体的检索交互成本；该效应取决于环境，且仅靠完成率无法察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准指标任务完成率无法揭示压缩带来的隐藏交互成本，因为压缩可能迫使智能体重新获取已丢弃的执行相关状态，而完成率统计上不变。

Method: 在固定24回合的确定性规划环境中，通过改变压缩强度、对比丢弃与保留事实操作符、利用预言机干预恢复状态，并将工具调用分解为检索与执行，在三个模型、两个任务场景及ALFWorld环境中测量重获取成本。

Result: 所有六项模型-场景比较中检索调用均增加，且几乎解释了全部新增交互；五/六在Holm校正后显著。5倍压缩时完成率无显著变化；GPT-5.5完成率从80%升至85%（p=1.0），检索从21.0增至63.9（p=.002）。保留干预显示语义无关内容使检索增加57%（p<.001）而完成率不变；ALFWorld中未出现检索激增。

Conclusion: 当执行相关状态缺失并被重新获取时，压缩会产生隐藏的交互成本，仅凭任务完成率无法暴露这些成本；评估压缩应考虑检索等交互成本。

中文摘要：任务完成率是评估上下文压缩的标准指标，但它并不完备：压缩可能通过迫使智能体重新获取已丢弃的状态来增加其交互成本，而完成率在统计上保持不变。我们引入了一种受控运行时测量协议，用于在有界视界的使用工具的智能体中测量重获取成本。该智能体在确定性规划环境中运行，固定24回合视界。我们改变压缩强度，比较丢弃操作符与保留事实的操作符，通过受控的预言机干预恢复已丢弃状态，并将工具调用分解为检索与执行两部分。我们评估了三个模型在两个任务场景下的表现。在全部六个模型-场景比较中，检索调用均增加，且几乎解释了所有新增交互；其中五个在Holm校正后仍显著。在预设的5倍比较点，任何单元格中的完成率变化均不显著。DeepSeek仅在10倍压缩时表现出显著的完成率下降。GPT-5.5是最清晰的案例：完成率从80%变为85%（p = 1.0），而检索调用从21.0增至63.9（p = .002）。保留干预进一步区分了状态数量、状态类型和内容有效性。随机选择与离线事后预言机相当，而将保留的D状态替换为语义无关内容会使检索增加57%（p < .001），且完成率无显著变化。在第二个环境ALFWorld中，滑动压缩未产生检索激增，表明重获取特征取决于环境，而非上下文缩短本身所固有。总体而言，当与执行相关的状态缺失且必须被重新获取时，压缩会带来隐藏的交互成本，而仅靠完成率可能无法暴露这些成本。

Original abstract: Task completion is the standard metric for evaluating context compression, yet it is incomplete: compression can increase an agent's interaction cost by forcing it to reacquire dropped state while leaving completion statistically unchanged.
  We introduce a controlled runtime measurement protocol for reacquisition cost in a bounded-horizon tool-using agent. The agent acts in a deterministic planning environment under a fixed 24-turn horizon. We vary compression severity, compare a dropping operator with a fact-preserving operator, restore dropped state through controlled oracle interventions, and decompose tool calls into retrieval and execution. We evaluate three models across two task regimes.
  Retrieval calls increase in all six model-regime comparisons and account for almost all added interaction; five of six remain significant after Holm correction. At the prespecified 5x comparison point, completion changes are not significant in any cell. DeepSeek shows a significant completion drop only at 10x compression. GPT-5.5 is the clearest case: completion changes from 80% to 85% (p = 1.0) while retrieval increases from 21.0 to 63.9 calls (p = .002).
  Retention interventions further separate state quantity, state type, and content validity. Random selection is comparable to an offline hindsight oracle, while replacing retained D-state with semantically irrelevant content increases retrieval by 57% (p < .001) without a significant completion change. In a second environment, ALFWorld, sliding compression produces no retrieval surge, showing that the reacquisition signature is environment-dependent rather than intrinsic to shortening context.
  Overall, compression can impose hidden interaction costs when execution-relevant state becomes absent and must be reacquired, while completion alone may not expose those costs.

</details>


### [83] [AstronOS: A Unified Execution Model and Runtime for Long-Horizon Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16381)
*Zhenhang Nie,Gui Zheng,Xudong Sun,Tailong Zhu,Bin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 2 figures, 13 tables. Zhenhang Nie and Gui Zheng contributed equally; Gui Zheng and Bin Zhang are corresponding authors

Selection reason: 提出长程 agent 的统一执行模型与运行时，用受控实验对比跨会话状态交接策略，直接为多阶段代码优化 agent 的执行架构提供借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的执行模型，通过持久身份和版本化状态管理跨调用工作项；在AstronOS中实现后，相比重读、重放历史、摘要和JSON等方法，显著提高了新会话中的端到端通过率，尽管增加了时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理系统通常将执行和状态绑定在单一对话或代理实例上，难以应对跨越多次调用和阶段的实际工作，导致新会话中无法有效延续既定计划。

Method: 引入统一执行模型，维护工作项的持久身份和版本化权威状态；在AstronOS中通过Cases、Tasks和Scenario Packs实现选定路径，并对比五种策略（重读、重放历史、文本摘要、JSON、AstronOS中介交接）在受控任务上的效果。

Result: 在主要三阶段批次中，AstronOS通过率14/15，远高于重读0/15和历史重放2/15；其每次通过执行的令牌成本更低，但执行窗口时间更长。

Conclusion: 完整的AstronOS条件在跨新会话的基准测试中实现了更高的端到端通过率，但需要付出可测量的时间成本。

中文摘要：代理系统通常围绕单个对话、模型调用或代理实例来组织执行和状态，即使实际工作跨越多次调用和多个阶段。我们引入了一种统一的执行模型，该模型在工作项的多次调用中保持其持久身份和版本化的权威状态。每个步骤接收特定于某个状态版本和新材料的输入；结果只有在验证和记录之后才能推进状态。我们在AstronOS中通过Cases、Tasks和Scenario Packs在中央和本地执行中实现了该模型的选定路径。我们将五种完整的策略进行比较，以将既定的软件版本更新计划带入新的模型会话：重读原始材料、重放完整历史、确定性文本摘要、确定性JSON以及AstronOS运行时中介的交接。在五种策略下运行十个受控任务，每次重复三次，共产生150次包含的执行。在单阶段参考族中，各策略表现相似。在主要的三阶段A-C批次中，AstronOS在15次执行中有14次通过了冻结评分器，而重读为0/15，完整历史重放为2/15；后来的非交错摘要和JSON批次各为0/15。AstronOS在每次通过执行中具有更低的基于尝试次数的模型令牌成本，但每次尝试需要更多的执行窗口时间。这些结果将完整的AstronOS条件与该基准中跨新会话的更高端到端通过率联系起来，但代价是可测量的时间成本。

Original abstract: Agentic systems often organize execution and state around a single conversation, model invocation, or agent instance, even when real work spans many calls and stages. We introduce a unified execution model that maintains a work item's persistent identity and versioned authoritative state across calls. Each step receives input scoped to a specific state version and new material; a result advances state only after validation and recording. We implement selected paths of this model in AstronOS using Cases, Tasks, and Scenario Packs across central and local execution. We compare five complete strategies for carrying an established software-version update plan into a fresh model session: rereading original materials, replaying full history, deterministic text summary, deterministic JSON, and the AstronOS runtime-mediated handoff. Ten controlled tasks are run under all five strategies with three repetitions, yielding 150 included executions. On the single-stage reference family, strategies perform similarly. In the primary three-stage A-C batch, AstronOS passes the frozen scorer in 14 of 15 executions, compared with 0 of 15 for rereading and 2 of 15 for full-history replay; later non-interleaved summary and JSON batches each pass 0 of 15. AstronOS has lower attempt-accounted model-token cost per passing execution, while requiring more execution-window time per attempt. These results associate the complete AstronOS condition with higher end-to-end pass rates across fresh sessions in this benchmark, at a measurable time cost.

</details>


### [84] [A Policy Algebra for Trust-Preserving Agentic AI Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16402)
*Bhaskar Tripathi,Anurag Kumar,Ramendra Kumar,Bhavesh Gadhe*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 figures, 10 tables, and 5 algorithms

Selection reason: 给出 agent 执行中的策略代数与可靠性包络，约束能力与信任的权衡，可用于 coding agent 的权限、审计与副作用控制。

TL;DR: 提出一种策略代数框架，为智能体定义可靠性边界，在保证高任务完成率的同时大幅减少策略违规并增强审计完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体框架只优化能力，但企业执行需要可靠性——结果必须通过合规、可审计的动作路径产生，而不仅是完成任务。

Method: 提出策略代数，通过组合安全配置和运行时义务（如连接、交集、预算缩窄、审批继承、证据积累）定义可靠性包络，并跨多智能体传播限制，引入成本感知工件物化。

Result: 干预94.8%的违反策略事件，保留86.9%的任务完成率，消除配置单调性和零工件耗尽违规，审计完整性提升至98.6%。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了形式化正确性条件、可执行语义和追踪证据，使智能体不仅有能力，而且可靠有能力，适合企业级部署。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型的智能体框架主要优化能力：智能体是否能够推理、检索信息、调用工具、委派工作并完成目标。企业级执行需要更强的性质。如果成功的结果是通过未经授权的数据访问、扩大的委派权限、未经批准的副作用、不可恢复的预算消耗或不完整的证据产生的，那么该结果并不可靠。本文将可靠能力定义为路径性质：只有当智能体通过符合身份、配置文件、工具、数据、记忆、预算、工件、审批和审计约束的可允许动作事件来完成任务时，它才是可靠有能力的。我们提出了一种策略代数，定义了智能体能力可被行使的可靠性边界。安全配置文件和运行时义务通过连接、交集、预算缩窄、审批继承和证据积累进行组合；所得组合既保持信任，又是在满足所有治理输入的情况下限制性最小的状态。该代数还跨多智能体调用传播限制，并引入成本感知的工件物化，随着预算暴露的增长，将开放式执行导向可恢复的结果。评估被解释为可靠性-能力权衡而非能力基准：策略代数运行时干预了94.8%的违反策略事件，同时保留了86.9%的任务完成率，消除了观察到的配置文件单调性和零工件耗尽违规，并将审计完整性提高到98.6%。该方法为研究人员和实践者提供了形式化正确性条件、可执行决策语义和痕迹证据，以构建不仅有能力而且可靠有能力的智能体。

Original abstract: Large language model-based agentic frameworks primarily optimize capability: whether an agent can reason, retrieve information, call tools, delegate work, and complete a goal. Enterprise execution requires a stronger property. A successful result is not reliable if it was produced through unauthorized data access, widened delegated authority, unapproved side effects, unrecoverable budget consumption, or incomplete evidence. This paper defines reliable capability as a path property: an agent is reliably capable only when it completes a task through action events that remain admissible under identity, profile, tool, data, memory, budget, artifact, approval, and audit constraints. We propose a policy algebra that defines the reliability envelope within which agent capability may be exercised. Security profiles and runtime obligations compose through joins, intersections, budget narrowing, approval inheritance, and evidence accumulation; the resulting composition is both trust-preserving and the least restrictive state satisfying all governing inputs. The algebra also propagates restrictions across multi-agent calls and introduces cost-aware artifact materialization, which redirects open-ended execution toward a recoverable outcome as budget exposure grows. The evaluation is interpreted as a reliability-capability trade-off rather than a capability benchmark: the policy-algebra runtime intervenes on 94.8% of policy-violating events while retaining an 86.9% task-completion rate, eliminates the observed profile-monotonicity and zero-artifact-exhaustion violations, and increases audit completeness to 98.6%. The method provides researchers and practitioners with formal correctness conditions, executable decision semantics, and trace evidence for building agents that are not only capable, but reliably capable.

</details>


### [85] [ParaTempo: Efficient Parallel Reasoning via Temporal Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16425)
