*Xuteng Zhang,Wenhao Zeng,Xiaodong Gu,Chao Hu,Haotian Lin,Yuling Shi,Min Wang,Beijun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ScottZhang812/ParaTempo

Selection reason: 提出基于时间置信度的并行推理分支剪枝与算力重分配，训练无关且动态控制探索开销，可迁移到 agent 的并行候选搜索与验证循环。

TL;DR: ParaTempo是一个无需训练的异步并行推理框架，利用时间置信度（分支级答案收敛度量）动态控制推理过程，在保持准确率的同时显著降低延迟和令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行推理虽然能提高准确性和鲁棒性，但计算成本高昂。现有控制方法（如最终答案共识、局部令牌置信度、孤立中间探针）存在信号延迟、与实际推理进展关联弱或噪声大等问题，难以实现动态的分支级控制。

Method: ParaTempo通过定期探测每个分支的暂定答案概率分布，计算时间置信度（即近期中间探针在主导答案上的集中程度）。基于该单一信号，动态执行剪枝低置信度分支、提前退出持续承诺的分支、分叉新分支重新分配计算资源，并在置信度加权投票集中时全局停止。整个过程无需分支间同步。

Result: 在数学和科学推理基准上，ParaTempo将平均延迟降低21.8%-32.2%，总令牌使用量降低18.1%-30.3%，同时保持与基线相当的准确率。时间置信度在时间稳定性和对未来分支收敛的预测能力上优于令牌级和瞬时信号。

Conclusion: ParaTempo验证了基于时间置信度的分支级异步控制能够高效管理并行推理资源，实现计算成本与推理精度的良好权衡，为大规模推理模型的推理加速提供了新思路。

中文摘要：并行推理通过探索多条解决方案路径来提高大型推理模型的准确性和鲁棒性，但其计算成本随推理深度和分支数量而增长。现有管理这些并行路径的方法通常依赖于最终答案共识、局部令牌置信度或孤立的中间探针。然而，这些信号往往滞后、与实际推理进展关联较弱，或者对于动态的分支级控制而言过于嘈杂。为了解决这些局限性，我们引入了ParaTempo，一种无需训练的异步并行推理框架。ParaTempo由时间置信度驱动，这是一种分支局部的答案空间收敛度量。每个分支被定期探测以获得暂定的答案概率分布，时间置信度量化了最近的中间探针在主导答案上的集中程度。一旦积累了足够的证据，ParaTempo就仅凭这一单一信号驱动其整个控制过程：低置信度分支被剪枝，持续坚持其主导答案的分支被提前退出，释放的计算资源通过分叉新分支重新分配，并且当置信度加权投票集中时，生成全局停止。无需推理轨迹之间的同步，ParaTempo根据分支级收敛自适应分配计算。在具有挑战性的数学和科学推理基准上的实验表明，ParaTempo将平均延迟降低了21.8%-32.2%，总令牌使用量降低了18.1%-30.3%，同时保持了具有竞争力的准确性。此外，时间置信度在时间稳定性和对未来分支收敛的预测能力方面优于令牌级和瞬时信号。

Original abstract: Parallel reasoning improves the accuracy and robustness of large reasoning models by exploring multiple solution paths, but its computational cost grows with reasoning depth and branch count. Existing methods for managing these parallel paths typically rely on final-answer consensus, local token confidence, or isolated intermediate probes. However, these signals are often delayed, weakly tied to actual reasoning progress, or too noisy for dynamic, branch-level control. To address these limitations, we introduce ParaTempo, a training-free asynchronous parallel reasoning framework. ParaTempo is driven by temporal confidence, a branch-local measure of answer-space convergence. Each branch is periodically probed for a tentative answer probability distribution, and temporal confidence quantifies how sharply the recent intermediate probes concentrate on a dominant answer. Once sufficient evidence has accumulated, ParaTempo drives its entire control process from this single signal: low-confidence branches are pruned, branches that persistently commit to their dominant answer are retired early, freed computation is reallocated by forking new branches, and generation stops globally once the confidence-weighted vote concentrates. Without requiring synchronization among reasoning trajectories, ParaTempo adaptively allocates computation based on branch-level convergence. Experiments on challenging mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks show that ParaTempo reduces average latency by 21.8-32.2% and total token usage by 18.1-30.3% while maintaining competitive accuracy. Moreover, temporal confidence exhibits stronger temporal stability and predictive power for future branch convergence than token-level and instantaneous signals.

</details>


### [86] [HaReCAP: Habitual-action Grounding for Recursive Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16447)
*Shen Liu,Zhenguo Xu,Shaopu Wang,Yike Gao,Chunlei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 在 ReCAP 递归长程 agent 中离线提炼可审计的叶子规则以跳过常规 LLM 调用，显著降 token，是轨迹经验复用与成本优化的直接示范。

TL;DR: 提出HaReCAP，通过离线编译可审计、可弃权的叶反射规则，在满足唯一合法动作时跳过叶节点LLM调用，降低ReCAP类递归框架的最后一英里落地冗余，在Robotouille和ALFWorld上节省14.67%-20.08% token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReCAP等递归上下文管理方法虽通过多级分解和父节点细化提升规划稳定性，但每个叶节点仍需反复调用LLM将原子子任务落地为精确动作，形成最后一英里落地冗余，在长时程执行中累积为大量LLM调用与token开销。

Method: HaReCAP从成功轨迹中提取高频叶节点决策，离线编译为一组可审计且可弃权的单步叶反射规则；运行时仅在规则能从当前有效动作集合唯一确定合法动作时跳过叶节点LLM调用，否则回退到原始ReCAP，从而在保留递归控制流的同时减少冗余。

Result: 在Robotouille和ALFWorld上以Qwen3.5-27B为主模型，对于ReCAP和HaReCAP均能解决的任务，token消耗在Robotouille同步、Robotouille异步和ALFWorld上分别降低14.67%、17.93%和20.08%。

Conclusion: HaReCAP可作为ReCAP风格递归上下文管理框架的低侵入式扩展，在常见成功轨迹上跨环境和模型有效减少最后一英里落地冗余，且不影响原始递归流程。

中文摘要：长时程具身任务要求LLM智能体迭代地分解高层目标、根据环境反馈修订计划，并将叶级子目标落地为有效可执行动作。诸如ReCAP的递归上下文管理方法通过多级任务分解和父节点细化提高了规划稳定性，但仍会在叶节点重复调用LLM，将原子子任务落地为精确有效动作。我们将这最后一步落地过程称为“最后一英里落地冗余”（last-mile grounding redundancy），它在长时程执行过程中累积为大量的LLM调用和token开销。为缓解该问题，我们提出HaReCAP（Habitual-action Grounded ReCAP），一种针对ReCAP的低侵入式叶节点落地扩展。HaReCAP从成功轨迹中提取频繁出现的叶节点决策，并在离线状态下将其编译为可审计且可弃权（abstainable）的单步叶反射规则。在运行时，仅当某条规则能在当前有效动作集合中唯一确定一个合法动作时，它才跳过叶节点LLM调用；否则回退到原始ReCAP。该设计避免了在常规叶节点动作落地时反复将完整递归上下文携带给LLM，同时保留了原始递归控制流。我们在Robotouille和ALFWorld上以Qwen3.5-27B作为主模型评估HaReCAP。在ReCAP和HaReCAP均能解决的任务上，HaReCAP在Robotouille同步、Robotouille异步和ALFWorld上分别将token消耗降低了14.67%、17.93%和20.08%。结果表明，HaReCAP可以作为ReCAP风格递归上下文管理框架的一种低侵入式扩展，在常见成功轨迹上跨环境和模型减少最后一英里落地冗余。

Original abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks require LLM agents to iteratively decompose high-level goals, revise plans in response to environmental feedback, and ground leaf-level subgoals into valid executable actions. Recursive context-management methods such as ReCAP improve planning stability through multi-level task decomposition and parent-node refinement, but still repeatedly invoke the LLM at leaf nodes to ground atomic subtasks into exact valid actions. We refer to this final grounding step as last-mile grounding redundancy, which accumulates into substantial LLM-call and token overhead during long-horizon execution. To mitigate this issue, we propose HaReCAP (Habitual-action Grounded ReCAP), a low-intrusion leaf grounding extension for ReCAP. HaReCAP extracts frequent leaf decisions from successful trajectories and compiles them offline into auditable and abstainable one-step leaf-reflex rules. At runtime, it skips the leaf LLM call only when a rule can uniquely determine a legal action in the current valid-action set; otherwise, it falls back to the original ReCAP. This design avoids repeatedly carrying the full recursive context into the LLM for routine leaf action grounding, while preserving the original recursive control flow. We evaluate HaReCAP on Robotouille and ALFWorld with Qwen3.5-27B as the main model. On tasks solved by both ReCAP and HaReCAP, HaReCAP reduces token consumption by 14.67%, 17.93%, and 20.08% on Robotouille synchronous, Robotouille asynchronous, and ALFWorld, respectively. The results show that HaReCAP can serve as a low-intrusion extension to ReCAP-style recursive context-management frameworks, reducing last-mile grounding redundancy across environments and models on commonly successful trajectories.

</details>


### [87] [JailbreakSkill: Scaling Automated Red-Teaming with Reusable and Ever-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16465)
*Xiaoyu Wen,Jiajia Li,Zhida He,Peng Yu,Chenxu Wang,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Cheng Jin,Ying Wen,Xingcheng Xu,Shuyue Hu,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出可复用且持续进化的技能库驱动自动化红队，技能迭代与跨任务复用的机制可迁移到 coding agent 的经验库与自我改进。

TL;DR: JailbreakSkill通过可复用且持续演进的技能库，将攻击策略模块化并闭环攻击与学习，显著提升自动化红队测试的规模化与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队攻击策略分散在提示词和工作流中，难以系统性整合、复用和规模化改进，限制了攻击能力的积累与提升。

Method: 提出JailbreakSkill框架：将攻击策略封装为模块化、智能体就绪的技能，支持跨任务/模型复用与自适应选择；并通过攻击-学习闭环，利用经验诊断、改进、组合和发现新技能，不断扩充技能库。

Result: 在AdvBench上宏平均ASR提升17.5个百分点，HarmBench上提升13.4个百分点；在AdvBench上对GPT-5.4提升48.6个百分点；产生并发现新攻击策略，且演化技能无需额外适配即可泛化到未见提示和目标模型。

Conclusion: JailbreakSkill通过可复用、持续演进的技能库闭环攻击与学习，为规模化自动化红队测试提供了高效路径，并已开源代码。

中文摘要：自动化红队测试已产生越来越多的攻击策略，但它们通常分散在各种提示词和工作流中，难以系统地整合、复用和规模化改进。我们引入了JailbreakSkill，一个以技能为中心的框架，通过可复用且持续演进的攻击能力来扩展自动化红队测试。JailbreakSkill将现有攻击策略封装为模块化、智能体就绪的技能，可直接复用，并可跨任务和目标模型自适应选择。除了复用之外，它还闭环了攻击与学习：利用攻击经验来诊断、改进、组合和发现新技能，并将其添加回不断增长的技能库中。这种演进使AdvBench上的宏平均ASR提升了17.5个百分点，HarmBench上提升了13.4个百分点，其中包括在AdvBench上针对GPT-5.4的48.6个百分点的提升，同时产生了新颖的攻击策略，例如将直接请求重新表述为未完成的文档完成任务。若干演化出的技能还能无需进一步适配即可泛化到未见过的提示词和目标模型。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/BattleWen/JailbreakSkill 获取。

Original abstract: Automated red-teaming has produced a growing collection of attack strategies, yet they typically remain scattered across prompts and workflows, making them difficult to systematically integrate, reuse, and improve at scale. We introduce \textsc{JailbreakSkill}, a skill-centric framework for scaling automated red-teaming through reusable and continuously evolving attack capabilities. \textsc{JailbreakSkill} packages existing attack strategies into modular, agent-ready skills that can be directly reused and adaptively selected across tasks and target models. Beyond reuse, it closes the loop between attacking and learning: attack experience is used to diagnose, refine, combine, and discover new skills, which are added back to an ever-growing skill library. This evolution lifts macro-average ASR by 17.5 percentage points on AdvBench and 13.4 points on HarmBench, including a 48.6-point gain against GPT-5.4 on AdvBench, while yielding novel attack strategies such as reframing a direct request as an unfinished document-completion task. Several evolved skills also generalize to unseen prompts and target models without further adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/BattleWen/JailbreakSkill.

</details>


### [88] [Physics of Agents: Statistical Mechanics Predicts Collective Behavior of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16578)
*Batu El,Jinhee Paeng,Fatih Dinc,Shiye Su,Mete Erdogan,Aneesh Pappu,Haotian Ye,Wanjia Zhao,Surya Ganguli,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 51 pages, 20 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 用统计力学预测大规模 LLM agent 群体的共识与极化动态，揭示多 agent 集体行为规律，可用于多 agent 协作设计。

TL;DR: 研究了超过10,000个语言模型代理社区的集体动态，发现其行为可归结为冷漠、极化和共识三种机制，并提出一个统计力学模型，仅凭初始观点即可预测个体轨迹并解释集体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理越来越多地以交互系统形式运作，其集体动态可能导致求真或极化等不同结果，理解并预测这些动态对设计有效对齐的多代理系统至关重要。

Method: 对超过10,000个语言模型代理社区进行实验，让代理在客观数学和主观政治问题上反复交换消息并修正观点，提出一个统计力学模型，其中代理随机倾向于较低的社会压力，并利用拟合模型参数分析集体行为机制。

Result: 模型仅凭初始观点即可预测个体轨迹，优于所有标准基线，泛化到未见社区图，并再现群体原型分布；发现社区低于临界社会温度、吸引力纽带占优、正确回答者影响力最大。

Conclusion: AI代理的集体行为遵循紧凑且可预测的动力学定律，类似于其他复杂系统，为理解和设计多代理系统提供了理论基础。

中文摘要：AI代理越来越多地作为交互系统的一部分而非孤立地运作。随着代理交换信息并共同做出决策，它们的交互可以改善集体推理，但也可能产生羊群效应、极化或放大共同的偏见。因此，理解和预测这些集体动态对于设计有效且对齐的多代理系统至关重要。在这里，我们研究了超过10,000个语言模型代理社区，这些代理在客观数学问题和主观政治陈述中反复交换消息并修正自己的观点。尽管可能的行为存在显著多样性，个体和群体动态可以用三个特征机制来表征：冷漠、极化和共识。AI代理开始时漠不关心，并在交互中建立信念。在客观问题上，沟通提高了集体准确性，而在主观问题上，它常常使群体观点向右翼政治光谱漂移。我们用一种统计力学形式主义来解释这些观察，其中代理随机地偏好较低的社会压力。仅给定初始观点，我们的模型预测个体轨迹，优于所有标准基线，泛化到未见过的社区图，并再现观察到的群体原型分布。我们拟合的模型参数揭示了关键观察背后的机制：i) 社区运行在临界社会温度以下，这解释了信念的建立；ii) 吸引力纽带超过排斥力纽带，这有利于共识；iii) 持有正确答案的代理施加最强的拉力，这驱动了求真。总的来说，我们的结果表明，AI代理的集体行为与其他复杂系统一样，遵循紧凑且可预测的动力学定律。

Original abstract: AI agents increasingly operate as part of interacting systems rather than in isolation. As agents exchange information and jointly make decisions, their interactions can improve collective reasoning but may also produce herding, polarization, or amplify shared biases. Understanding and predicting these collective dynamics is therefore important for designing effective and aligned multi-agent systems. Here, we study over 10,000 communities of language-model agents that repeatedly exchange messages and revise their opinions across objective mathematics questions and subjective political statements. Despite substantial diversity in possible behavior, the individual and group dynamics can be represented by three characteristic regimes: indifference, polarization, and consensus. AI agents start indifferent and build conviction as they interact. On objective questions, communication improves collective accuracy, while on subjective questions it often drifts group opinions toward the right in the political spectrum. We explain these observations with a statistical-mechanics formalism in which agents stochastically favor lower social pressure. Given only initial opinions, our model predicts individual trajectories, outperforms all standard baselines, generalizes to unseen community graphs, and reproduces the observed group archetype distributions. Our fitted model parameters reveal the mechanics underlying our key observations: i) communities operate below the critical social temperature, which explains conviction buildup; ii) attractive ties outweigh repulsive ones, which favors consensus; and iii) agents holding the correct answer exert the strongest pull, which drives truth-seeking. Overall, our results demonstrate that collective behavior of AI agents, like that of other complex systems, follows compact and predictive dynamical laws.

</details>


### [89] [PDDLCoder: Agentic PDDL Generation for LLM-Assisted Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16637)
*Veit Laule,Jiangtao Shuai,Manfred Hauswirth,Sonja Schimmler*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出agentic PDDL生成框架，通过迭代生成-分析-精炼与可执行环境验证，对LLM规划agent有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出PDDLCoder智能体框架与NL-pddlgym基准，在106个测试问题上实现89.6%的适用计划生成，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长时程规划中不可靠，混合方法依赖刚性流程、部分PDDL定义或人工反馈，且缺少标准化自动验证基准。

Method: PDDLCoder通过迭代生成、分析并修正PDDL规划规范，并构建含23个域711个规划问题及可执行gym环境的NL-pddlgym基准。

Result: 在保留的4个域106个问题上，PDDLCoder生成适用计划达89.6%，优于先前PDDL方法最高45.3%和直接LLM规划最高74.5%。

Conclusion: 智能体式PDDL生成对规划任务有效，并提供了可复现的基准以促进LLM辅助符号规划研究。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）在长时程规划中仍然不可靠，常常生成逻辑不一致或不可适用的计划。最近的混合方法转而将自然语言翻译为规划领域定义语言（PDDL），使符号规划器能够生成可验证的计划。然而，现有方法常常依赖僵化的生成流程、部分PDDL定义或人类反馈。此外，由于缺乏具有自动验证功能的标准化基准，其评估受到阻碍。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了PDDLCoder，一个从自然语言生成PDDL的智能体框架，它迭代地生成、分析和改进规划规范。我们还引入了NL-pddlgym，一个基准数据集，包含23个领域的711个规划问题，并提供可执行的gym环境，用于自动验证计划适用性。在包含4个保留领域共106个问题的NL-pddlgym测试集上的实验表明，PDDLCoder能为89.6%的测试规划问题生成适用计划。这优于我们对先前PDDL生成方法的改编（最高达到45.3%），并超过了直接LLM规划方法（在同一测试集上最高达到74.5%）。我们的工作展示了智能体式PDDL生成在规划中的有效性，并为未来LLM辅助符号规划研究建立了可复现的基准。

Original abstract: LLMs remain unreliable for long-horizon planning, often generating logically inconsistent or non-applicable plans. Recent hybrid methods instead translate natural language into the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL), allowing symbolic planners to produce verifiable plans. However, existing methods frequently rely on rigid generation pipelines, a partial PDDL definition, or human feedback. Furthermore, their evaluation is hindered by the lack of standardized benchmarks with automated verification. To address these limitations, we present PDDLCoder, an agentic framework for PDDL generation from natural language that iteratively generates, analyzes, and refines planning specifications. We further introduce NL-pddlgym, a benchmark dataset comprising 711 planning problems across 23 domains with executable gym environments for the automated verification of plan applicability. Experiments on the NL-pddlgym test set containing 106 problems across 4 held-out domains show that PDDLCoder generates applicable plans for 89.6\% of tested planning problems. This improves upon our adaptations of previous PDDL generation methods, which achieved up to 45.3\%, and outperforms direct LLM planning approaches, which reached up to 74.5\% on the same test set. Our work demonstrates the effectiveness of agentic PDDL generation for planning and establishes a reproducible benchmark for future research on LLM-assisted symbolic planning.

</details>


### [90] [Reconstruction: A Blind Benchmark for Recovering Research Ideas from Pre-Publication Bibliographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16645)
*Shaolong Chen,Yanlin Fei,Nazhou Liu,Xinmiao Yu,Lei Li,Rahul Thapa,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 盲基准评估多agent跨模型评审与锦标赛选择，涉及agent评测与协作流程，可迁移到coding agent评估设计。

TL;DR: 提出Reconstruction基准，仅凭参考文献恢复论文想法；多智能体流程将匹配率从3-15%提升至23-42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估语言模型能否仅从论文发表前的参考文献中重建真实研究想法，并设计严格防泄漏的盲评估协议。

Method: 构建Reconstruction基准，隐藏种子论文及同期/未来文献，采用时间截断、匿名ID和冻结参考文献列表防泄漏；提出多智能体流程，结合跨模型评审和瑞士制锦标赛选择假设。

Result: 七个前沿模型匹配率仅3-15%；多智能体流程在六个领域将匹配率提升至23-42%，约为最佳单模型的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 仅凭参考文献恢复研究想法具有挑战性，但多智能体协作显著提升性能，该协议为模型推理评估提供了可扩展的盲测方案。

中文摘要：语言模型能否仅凭论文发表前的参考文献列表恢复已发表论文的真实研究想法？我们引入Reconstruction，一个盲想法恢复基准，它隐藏种子论文及所有同期或未来文献，并要求模型提出假设，由独立的大语言模型评审将其与留出的地面真实想法进行匹配。严格的防泄漏协议——时间引文截断、匿名参考ID、冻结每篇论文的参考文献列表——防止提示时泄漏种子想法。在六个科学领域和643篇评估论文中，七个前沿模型仅达到适度的匹配率（约3-15%）。然后我们评估一个仅参考的多智能体（前4）流程，该流程将跨模型评审与对齐假设槽位的瑞士制锦标赛相结合，无需外部网络搜索。跨模型评审加锦标赛选择将所有六个领域的匹配率提高到约23-42%，这是相对于最佳单模型基线观察到的大约2.4倍提升。本草案报告协议、防泄漏设计和当前结果，作为arXiv时间戳。

Original abstract: Can a language model recover the true research idea of a published paper when given only that paper's pre-publication bibliography? We introduce Reconstruction, a blind idea-recovery benchmark that withholds the seed paper and all contemporaneous or future literature, and asks models to propose hypotheses that an independent large language model judge matches against the held-out ground-truth idea. A strict anti-leakage protocol-temporal citation cutoff, anonymous reference IDs, and frozen per-paper bibliographies, which prevents prompt-time leakage of the seed idea. Across six scientific domains and 643 evaluated papers, seven frontier models achieve only modest Match rates (approx. 3-15%). We then evaluate a reference-only multi-agent (top 4) pipeline that combines cross-model review with a Swiss tournament over aligned hypothesis slots, without external web search. Cross-model review plus tournament selection raises Match rates to approx. 23-42% across all six domains, which is an observed approx. 2.4x lift over the best single-model baseline. This draft reports the protocol, anti-leakage design, and current results as an arXiv timestamp.

</details>


### [91] [Policy Iteration with Human Feedback: Bringing Post-Training RL to In-context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16831)
*Minh-Ha Nguyen,Cathy Shyr*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: PIHF method paper

Selection reason: 将策略迭代与人类反馈引入上下文学习，用版本化策略、批评者与专家审核实现持续改进，属通用自改进agent方法。

TL;DR: PIHF使用预训练语言模型作为执行器，通过语言模型评论家和临床专家循环审查与修订策略，在罕见病诊断中显著提升Recall@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成预训练和上下文学习的基础上，需要一个能持续改进策略的机制，使固定模型能通过专家反馈迭代优化，以应对罕见病诊断等复杂任务。

Method: PIHF采用预训练语言模型作为固定权重执行基底，维护版本化的自然语言策略和工具集；语言模型评论家与临床专家共同审查推理和工具使用轨迹，定位失败并提出候选修订，专家保留接纳和回滚权，并用Recall@1和Recall@5验证执行效果。

Result: 在累积消融和超罕见病基准上，PIHF策略在多个执行器上提升Recall@1：专有执行器GPT-5.4提升32.7个百分点，开放权重执行器Qwen3.6-35B提升31.1个百分点，两者相差1.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，使用预训练语言模型作为固定权重执行基底，结合专家引导的策略迭代开发，在罕见病诊断中是可行的。

中文摘要：生成预训练建立了可复用的任务表示；后来的基于语言的任务条件化和上下文学习工作表明，固定模型可以适应来自指令和演示的行为。策略迭代与人类反馈（PIHF）建立在这一发展以及广义策略迭代的循环评估-改进结构之上。PIHF使用预训练语言模型作为其执行基底，并将持续性修订转移到版本化的自然语言策略和工具集。一个语言模型评论家和临床专家审查完整面板推理和工具使用轨迹，以定位反复出现的失败并形成候选修订；专家可以重新解释证据，并保留接纳和回滚的权限，而Recall@1和Recall@5在候选执行后验证结果。在累积消融和超罕见疾病基准上，PIHF衍生的策略在一个专有执行器和三个开放权重执行器（涵盖30亿到490亿激活参数）中提高了Recall@1。GPT-5.4提升32.7个百分点，Qwen3.6-35B提升31.1个百分点，差异为1.7个百分点。这些结果支持使用预训练语言模型作为固定权重执行基底，在罕见病诊断中进行专家引导的策略开发的可行性。

Original abstract: Generative pretraining established reusable task representations; later work on language-based task conditioning and in-context learning showed that a fixed model could adapt its behavior from instructions and demonstrations. Policy Iteration with Human Feedback (PIHF) builds on this development and the recurrent evaluate-and-improve structure of generalized policy iteration. PIHF uses a pretrained language model as its execution substrate and moves persistent revision to a versioned natural-language policy and tool set. A language-model critic and clinical expert review complete-panel reasoning and tool-use trajectories to localize recurrent failures and form candidate revisions; the expert may reinterpret the evidence and retains authority over admission and rollback, while Recall@1 and Recall@5 validate outcomes after candidate execution.
  Across cumulative ablations and ultra-rare-disease benchmarks, a PIHF-derived policy improved Recall@1 in one proprietary executor and three open-weight executors spanning 3 to 49 billion active parameters. Gains were 32.7 percentage points for GPT-5.4 and 31.1 points for Qwen3.6-35B, a difference of 1.7 points. These results support the feasibility of using pretrained language models as fixed-weight execution substrates for expert-guided policy development in rare-disease diagnosis.

</details>


### [92] [What Do Compliance Detectors Read? An Audit of Activation Probes and Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16852)
*Saisab Sadhu,Aadit Sengupta,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 审计合规检测器的规则盲区并引入无训练ICS分数，对agent可靠性与安全评估有方法借鉴。

TL;DR: 研究表明现有合规检测器存在规则盲现象，即判定不依赖于具体规则；提出的ICS虽成本低但无法超越简单基线，需逐步推理才能解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管合规监控只有在检测器依据明确的书面规则而非场景表面特征做出判定时才有意义；现有检测器未能满足这一条件，存在规则盲。

Method: 通过反事实操作（删除、置换、替换规则）和交叉规则基准测试多个防护与探针；提出无需训练的ICS，用10个标注对校准单一投影，并审计多个模型和基准。

Result: 所有快速检测器都表现规则盲，ICS未达到预注册的击败平凡基线的标准，且词袋模型与ICS泛化相同；ICS虽提升排序通过率但被自适应白盒攻击消除。

Conclusion: 快速检测器无法逃脱规则盲，逐步推理是有效途径；ICS成本低但需配合更严谨评估，发布基准和协议供未来检测器验证。

中文摘要：在已部署语言模型中的监管合规监控日益被实施为一种法律和审计控制措施，根据涵盖数据保护、医疗保健、金融监管和平台政策的书面规则检查模型输出。这种监控只有在检测器的判定取决于所述规则而非场景的表面特征时才有意义。我们表明，这一条件在当前类别的合规检测器中不成立，我们将这种失败称为规则盲（rule blindness）。删除、置换或替换管辖规则，对我们测试的每个防护（guard）和激活探针（activation probe）的检测准确率都没有影响，包括一个策略条件化的防护，它能正确引用管辖条款，但当该条款被替换为其许可性对应条款时，其判定几乎不变。一个专门构建的基准将两条规则与两个场景交叉组合，使得单独任何一方都无法预测标签，从而在先前基准无法排除的设计下证实了这种失败，并表明逐步推理（而非我们测试的任何快速检测器）才能摆脱它。大规模审计需要一种无需重新训练的检测器，因此我们引入了内部合规分数（Internal Compliance Score, ICS）：一种无需训练的激活读数，由十个标注样本对校准，并通过单一投影评分。我们以与它所审计的防护相同的审视标准来要求ICS：预先注册的击败平凡基线的标准未达到，而词袋模型在池化泛化上与它完全匹配。它仍然有用，因为它成本低廉，使我们能够审计四个已部署的防护模型、一个8B零样本裁判和十三个基准，并且在用于对候选回答排序时提高了机械验证的通过率，尽管一种自适应白盒攻击消除了这一收益。我们发布反事实协议和交叉规则基准，以便未来的探针和防护声明可以测试规则盲。

Original abstract: Regulatory compliance monitoring in deployed language models is increasingly implemented as a legal and audit control, checking model outputs against written rules spanning data protection, healthcare, financial regulation, and platform policy. Such monitoring is meaningful only if a detector's verdict depends on the stated rule rather than on surface features of the scenario. We show this condition fails across the current class of compliance detectors, a failure we call rule blindness. Deleting, permuting, or substituting the governing rule leaves detection accuracy unchanged for every guard and activation probe we test, including a policy-conditioned guard that correctly cites the governing clause yet barely changes its verdict when that clause is swapped for its permissive counterpart. A purpose-built benchmark crossing two rules with two scenarios, so that neither alone predicts the label, confirms the failure under a design no prior benchmark rules out, and shows that step by step reasoning, not any fast detector we test, is what escapes it. Auditing at scale requires a retraining-free detector, so we introduce the Internal Compliance Score (ICS): a training-free activation readout calibrated from ten labelled pairs and scored by a single projection. We hold ICS to the same scrutiny as the guards it audits: a pre-registered criterion for beating trivial baselines is not met, and a bag-of-words model matches its pooled generalisation exactly. It remains useful because it is inexpensive, letting us audit four deployed guard models, an 8B zero-shot judge, and thirteen benchmarks, and it raises the mechanically verified pass rate when used to rank candidate responses, though an adaptive white-box attack removes this gain. We release the counterfactual protocol and crossed-rule benchmark so rule blindness can be tested in future probe and guard claims.

</details>

## SE 精选（SemOpt / Coding Agent 相关）

以下为从原始 cs.SE 列表中筛出的 SemOpt / Coding Agent 相关论文。

- [Evaluating Agentic Code Repair Capabilities in Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14863) — 相关度 0.95：首个面向分布式系统缺陷修复的 agent 评测基准，直接契合代码修复 agent 研究。
- [Kozuchi Agent: A Language-Agnostic Open-Weight Agent for Software Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15579) — 相关度 0.95：开源权重软件修复 agent 与 CI 评估流水线，在 SWE-bench 等基准上取得领先结果。
- [TDD-Agent: Test-Driven Reasoning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16742) — 相关度 0.95：将测试驱动开发思想引入代码生成 agent，提出双轨迭代改进框架。
- [LongRCA Bench: Diagnosing Responsible Roles and Root Causes in Long-Horizon Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15242) — 相关度 0.90：长轨迹 agent 失败根因归因基准，为复杂 agent 诊断提供评测与基线。
- [When Agents Coordinate: Measuring Coordination in Multi-Agent AI Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16801) — 相关度 0.90：提出多 agent 编程团队协调度的度量方法，并基于大规模运行揭示协调模式。
- [When Uncertainty Isn't Enough: An Empirical Study of Self-Correction in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14659) — 相关度 0.90：系统研究代码生成中不确定性估计与自纠正策略，对 agent 可靠性设计有直接帮助。
- [Towards Risk-free AI Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16411) — 相关度 0.85：系统阐述 agent 测试与调试的研究方向，为 agent 可靠性工程提供框架与清单。
- [When Agentic Executions Fail: Detecting and Localizing Runtime Faults from Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14680) — 相关度 0.85：从执行遥测中检测与定位 agent 运行时故障的基准，可迁移到 agent 调试与监控。
- [The Quality of Claude AI-authored Python Tests Is Not Weaker Than Human-authored Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15188) — 相关度 0.80：对比 AI 与人类编写测试质量的系统方法，对代码生成与测试 agent 有直接参考价值。
- [A Temporal Reasoning Benchmarking Framework for LRMs via Difficulty-controlled and Dynamic Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04784) — 相关度 0.60：时间推理benchmark框架，其难度控制与推理忠实度验证可借鉴到agent评测设计。
- [PandasCorpus: A Resource of Real-World Pandas Workflows and Usage Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14742) — 相关度 0.60：真实 Pandas 工作流语料，可复用为代码生成/组合的评测与训练数据来源。
- [ModBench: A Pipeline for Building Modelica Benchmark Datasets Mined from Library Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16638) — 相关度 0.50：从仓库挖掘基准数据集的流水线，其方法论可迁移到其他语言/系统的基准构建。
